    `Roth.main_lemma,
    `Roth.approxSet,

    `Roth.approxSet_den_le_finite,
    `Roth.eventually_div_rpow_lt,

    `Roth.abs_eval_lt_of_le_index,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.exists_roth_parameters_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.exists_roth_approximants_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.one_lt_roth_eval_bound_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.roth_product_bound_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.roth_log_bound_aux,
    `Roth.roth_of_isIntegral,
    `Roth.roth,
    `Roth.roth_rat,
    `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.IsDiophantine,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.LeanEval
      `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.exists_pos_div_rpow_lt_aux,
    `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.isDiophantine_iff,
    `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.thueSiegelRoth]
  let unique := names.toList.eraseDups.length
  unless names.size == 293 && unique == 293 do
    throwError "INDEX_BAD_CANONICAL_ARRAY"
  let mut present : Nat := 0
  let mut values : Nat := 0
  for n in names do
    match env.find? n with
    | none => throwError "INDEX_NOT_LOADED: {n}"
    | some ci =>
      present := present + 1
      match ci.value? (allowOpaque := true) with
      | none => pure ()
      | some val =>
        values := values + 1
        if val.getUsedConstants.any (fun c ↦ c == target) then
          Lean.logInfo m!"INDEX_DIRECT_CONSUMER: {n}"
  Lean.logInfo m!"INDEX_AUDIT: requested={names.size}; unique={unique}"
  Lean.logInfo m!"INDEX_AUDIT_PRESENT: present={present}; values={values}"
end IndexAudit
namespace IndexAssessment133
open MvPolynomial
universe u v
noncomputable def genericIndex {σ : Type u} {R : Type v} [CommSemiring R]
    (P : MvPolynomial σ R) (α : σ → R) (w : σ → ℝ) : ℝ :=
  sInf ((fun d : σ →₀ ℕ ↦ d.sum fun j n ↦ (n : ℝ) * w j) ''
    ((aeval (fun j ↦ X j + C (α j)) P).support : Set (σ →₀ ℕ)))
theorem directUnfold {σ : Type u} [Fintype σ] (P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ) (α : σ → ℝ)
    (r : σ → ℕ) : Roth.index P α r =
      sInf (Roth.weight r '' ((taylor α P).support : Set (σ →₀ ℕ))) := rfl
theorem genericSpecialization {σ : Type u} [Fintype σ] (P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ) (α : σ → ℝ)
    (r : σ → ℕ) : genericIndex P α (fun j ↦ (r j : ℝ)⁻¹) = Roth.index P α r := by
  unfold genericIndex Roth.index Roth.weight taylor
  congr 2
  funext d
  rw [Finsupp.sum_fintype _ _ (by simp)]
  simp only [div_eq_mul_inv]
theorem zero {σ : Type u} [Fintype σ] (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) :
    Roth.index 0 α r = 0 := by simp [Roth.index]
theorem zeroWeights {σ : Type u} [Fintype σ] (P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ) (α : σ → ℝ) :
    Roth.index P α (fun _ ↦ 0) = 0 := by
  by_cases hP : P = 0
  · subst P; simp [Roth.index]
  · have hne : ((taylor α P).support : Set (σ →₀ ℕ)).Nonempty := by simpa using hP
    have hw : Roth.weight (fun _ : σ ↦ 0) = fun _ ↦ (0 : ℝ) := by
      funext d
      simp only [Roth.weight, Nat.cast_zero, div_zero, Finset.sum_const_zero]
    rw [Roth.index, hw, hne.image_const]
    simp
theorem beta {σ : Type u} [Fintype σ] (P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ) (α : σ → ℝ)
    (r : σ → ℕ) : (fun Q ↦ Roth.index Q α r) P = Roth.index P α r := rfl
#synth ConditionallyCompleteLinearOrder ℝ
#synth Coe (MvPolynomial (Fin 2) ℝ) (MvPowerSeries (Fin 2) ℝ)
theorem universeProbe (α : ULift.{1} (Fin 2) → ℝ) (r : ULift.{1} (Fin 2) → ℕ) :
    Roth.index (0 : MvPolynomial (ULift.{1} (Fin 2)) ℝ) α r = 0 := by
  simp [Roth.index]
theorem fintypeIndependence {σ : Type u} (i j : Fintype σ) (P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ)
    (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) :
    @Roth.index σ i P α r = @Roth.index σ j P α r := by
  have : i = j := Subsingleton.elim _ _
  subst j
  rfl
theorem noAutomaticUnfold {σ : Type u} [Fintype σ] (P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ) (α : σ → ℝ)
    (r : σ → ℕ) : Roth.index P α r =
      sInf (Roth.weight r '' ((taylor α P).support : Set (σ →₀ ℕ))) := by
  fail_if_success simp only
  rfl
-- This is a project bridge, not an existing mathlib replacement.
theorem positiveWeightedOrder {σ : Type u} [Fintype σ] (P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ) (α : σ → ℝ)
    (r : σ → ℕ) (hr : ∀ j, 0 < r j) : Roth.index P α r =
    (((taylor α P : MvPowerSeries σ ℝ).weightedOrder
      (fun j ↦ (∏ i, r i) / r j)).toNat : ℝ) / ∏ i, r i := by
  by_cases hP : P = 0
  · subst P; simp [Roth.index]
  · exact Roth.index_eq_weightedOrder_div hP hr α
#check @AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree
#check @Finset.coe_inf'
#check @Finset.inf'_eq_csInf_image
#print axioms genericIndex
#print axioms directUnfold
#print axioms genericSpecialization
#print axioms zero
#print axioms zeroWeights
#print axioms beta
#print axioms universeProbe
#print axioms fintypeIndependence
#print axioms noAutomaticUnfold
#print axioms positiveWeightedOrder
end IndexAssessment133
namespace IndexConsumers133
open MvPolynomial
universe u
theorem finsetBridge {ι : Type u} (s : Finset ι) (w : ι → ℝ) :
    (s.inf (fun d ↦ (w d : WithTop ℝ))).untopD 0 =
      sInf (w '' (s : Set ι)) := by
  classical
  rcases s.eq_empty_or_nonempty with h | h
  · subst s
    simp
  · change (s.inf (WithTop.some ∘ w)).untopD 0 = _
    rw [← Finset.coe_inf' h w, Finset.inf'_eq_csInf_image]
    rfl

theorem targetBridge {σ : Type u} [Fintype σ] (P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ)
    (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) : Roth.index P α r =
    (AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree (fun d ↦ (Roth.weight r d : WithTop ℝ))
      (taylor α P)).untopD 0 := by
  exact (finsetBridge (taylor α P).support (Roth.weight r)).symm

theorem indexSetBridge {σ : Type u} [Fintype σ] (B : ℝ) (q r : σ → ℕ) :
    Roth.indexSet B q r =
      {x | ∃ P ∈ Roth.RSet B r, ∃ h : σ → ℤ, (∀ j, IsCoprime (h j) (q j : ℤ)) ∧
        (AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree (fun d ↦ (Roth.weight r d : WithTop ℝ))
          (taylor (fun j ↦ (h j : ℝ) / q j) (map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) P))).untopD 0 = x} := by
  unfold Roth.indexSet
  simp_rw [targetBridge]
#print axioms targetBridge
#print axioms indexSetBridge
end IndexConsumers133
namespace IndexExpanded133
open MvPolynomial
universe u
theorem weightBridge {σ : Type u} [Fintype σ] (r : σ → ℕ) (d : σ →₀ ℕ) :
    Finsupp.weight (R := ℕ) (fun j ↦ (r j : ℝ)⁻¹) d = Roth.weight r d := by
  simp only [Roth.weight, Finsupp.weight_eq_sum, nsmul_eq_mul, div_eq_mul_inv]
theorem fullyExpandedBridge {σ : Type u} [Fintype σ] (P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ)
    (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) : Roth.index P α r =
    (AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree
      (fun d : σ →₀ ℕ ↦ (Finsupp.weight (R := ℕ) (fun j ↦ (r j : ℝ)⁻¹) d : WithTop ℝ))
      (aeval (fun j ↦ X j + C (α j)) P)).untopD 0 := by
  simpa only [weightBridge, taylor] using IndexConsumers133.targetBridge P α r
#print axioms weightBridge
#print axioms fullyExpandedBridge
end IndexExpanded133

namespace IndexEqZero139
universe u v w
#check @Finset.inf_eq_bot_iff
#check @Finset.inf_le_iff
#check @Finset.le_inf_iff
#check @Finset.coe_inf'
theorem abstractLaw {A : Type u} {R : Type v} {T : Type w} [Semiring R]
    [LinearOrder T] [BoundedOrder T] [Nontrivial T] (D : A → T)
    (P : AddMonoidAlgebra R A) (a₀ : A) (hD : ∀ a, D a = ⊥ ↔ a = a₀) :
    AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree D P = ⊥ ↔ P.coeff a₀ ≠ 0 := by
  simpa only [AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree, hD, exists_eq_right, Finsupp.mem_support_iff]
    using (Finset.inf_eq_bot_iff (s := P.coeff.support) (f := D))
theorem projectedLaw {A : Type u} {R : Type v} [Semiring R]
    (D : A → ℝ) (P : AddMonoidAlgebra R A) (a₀ : A)
    (hs : P.coeff.support.Nonempty) (hD : ∀ a, 0 ≤ D a)
    (hz : ∀ a, D a = 0 ↔ a = a₀) :
    (AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree (fun a ↦ (D a : WithTop ℝ)) P).untopD 0 = 0 ↔
      P.coeff a₀ ≠ 0 := by
  classical
  change (P.coeff.support.inf (WithTop.some ∘ D)).untopD 0 = 0 ↔ _
  rw [← Finset.coe_inf' hs D, WithTop.untopD_coe]
  have hn : 0 ≤ P.coeff.support.inf' hs D :=
    (Finset.le_inf'_iff hs D).mpr fun a _ ↦ hD a
  rw [show P.coeff.support.inf' hs D = 0 ↔ P.coeff.support.inf' hs D ≤ 0 from
    ⟨fun h ↦ h.le, fun h ↦ h.antisymm hn⟩]
  simpa only [hz, exists_eq_right, Finsupp.mem_support_iff] using
    (show P.coeff.support.inf' hs D ≤ 0 ↔ ∃ a ∈ P.coeff.support, D a = 0 from by
      rw [Finset.inf'_le_iff]
      exact exists_congr fun a ↦ and_congr_right fun _ ↦
        ⟨fun h ↦ h.antisymm (hD a), fun h ↦ h.le⟩)
#print axioms abstractLaw
#print axioms projectedLaw
end IndexEqZero139

namespace IndexEqZero139
universe u v
theorem nonnegativeBridge {A : Type u} {R : Type v} [Semiring R]
    (D : A → ℝ) (P : AddMonoidAlgebra R A) (hD : ∀ a, 0 ≤ D a) :
    (AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree (fun a ↦ (D a : WithTop ℝ)) P).untopD 0 =
      (((AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree
        (fun a ↦ WithTop.some (NNReal.mk (D a) (hD a))) P).untopD 0 : NNReal) : ℝ) := by
  classical
  rcases P.coeff.support.eq_empty_or_nonempty with hs | hs
  · simp only [AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree, hs, Finset.inf_empty, WithTop.untopD_top,
      NNReal.coe_zero]
  · change (P.coeff.support.inf (WithTop.some ∘ D)).untopD 0 =
      (((P.coeff.support.inf
        (WithTop.some ∘ (fun a ↦ NNReal.mk (D a) (hD a)))).untopD 0 : NNReal) : ℝ)
    rw [← Finset.coe_inf' hs D,
      ← Finset.coe_inf' hs (fun a ↦ NNReal.mk (D a) (hD a)), WithTop.untopD_coe,
      WithTop.untopD_coe]
    exact (Finset.apply_inf'_eq_inf'_comp (f := fun a ↦ NNReal.mk (D a) (hD a)) hs
      (fun x : NNReal ↦ (x : ℝ))
      (fun _ _ ↦ rfl)).symm

theorem nonnegativeZeroBridge {A : Type u} {R : Type v} [Semiring R]
    (D : A → ℝ) (P : AddMonoidAlgebra R A) (hD : ∀ a, 0 ≤ D a)
    (hs : P.coeff.support.Nonempty) :
    (AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree (fun a ↦ (D a : WithTop ℝ)) P).untopD 0 = 0 ↔
      AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree
        (fun a ↦ WithTop.some (NNReal.mk (D a) (hD a))) P = ⊥ := by
  rw [nonnegativeBridge D P hD]
  change (((P.coeff.support.inf
    (WithTop.some ∘ (fun a ↦ NNReal.mk (D a) (hD a)))).untopD 0 : NNReal) : ℝ) = 0 ↔ _
  rw [← Finset.coe_inf' hs (fun a ↦ NNReal.mk (D a) (hD a)), WithTop.untopD_coe]
  change _ ↔ P.coeff.support.inf (WithTop.some ∘ (fun a ↦ NNReal.mk (D a) (hD a))) = 0
  rw [← Finset.coe_inf' hs (fun a ↦ NNReal.mk (D a) (hD a))]
  simp only [WithTop.coe_eq_zero, NNReal.coe_eq_zero]
#print axioms nonnegativeBridge
#print axioms nonnegativeZeroBridge
end IndexEqZero139

namespace IndexEqZeroTarget139
open MvPolynomial Roth
universe u
theorem originalReplacement {σ : Type u} [Fintype σ] {P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ}
    (hP : P ≠ 0) {r : σ → ℕ} (hr : ∀ j, 0 < r j) (α : σ → ℝ) :
    Roth.index P α r = 0 ↔ eval α P ≠ 0 := by
  rw [IndexConsumers133.targetBridge]
  have hs : (taylor α P).coeff.support.Nonempty := by simpa using hP
  have h := IndexEqZero139.projectedLaw (Roth.weight r) (taylor α P) 0 hs
    (Roth.weight_nonneg r) (fun d ↦ Roth.weight_eq_zero_iff hr)
  change _ = 0 ↔ (taylor α P).coeff 0 ≠ 0 at h
  simpa only [coeff_taylor, hasseDeriv_zero_apply] using h

theorem zeroPolynomialGuard :
    Roth.index (0 : MvPolynomial Unit ℝ) (fun _ ↦ 0) (fun _ ↦ 1) = 0 ∧
      eval (fun _ ↦ 0) (0 : MvPolynomial Unit ℝ) = 0 := by
  simp [Roth.index]

theorem zeroWeightGuard :
    Roth.index (X () : MvPolynomial Unit ℝ) (fun _ ↦ 0) (fun _ ↦ 0) = 0 ∧
      eval (fun _ ↦ 0) (X () : MvPolynomial Unit ℝ) = 0 ∧
      (X () : MvPolynomial Unit ℝ) ≠ 0 := by
  simp [Roth.index, Roth.weight, support_X]

theorem mixedZeroWeightGuard :
    Roth.index (X false : MvPolynomial Bool ℝ) (fun _ ↦ 0)
      (fun b ↦ if b then 1 else 0) = 0 ∧
      eval (fun _ ↦ 0) (X false : MvPolynomial Bool ℝ) = 0 ∧
      (X false : MvPolynomial Bool ℝ) ≠ 0 := by
  simp [Roth.index, Roth.weight, taylor_X, support_X]
#print axioms originalReplacement
#print axioms zeroPolynomialGuard
#print axioms zeroWeightGuard
#print axioms mixedZeroWeightGuard
end IndexEqZeroTarget139

namespace IndexEqZeroTarget139
open MvPolynomial Roth
open scoped NNReal
universe u
theorem boundedReplacement {σ : Type u} [Fintype σ] {P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ}
    (hP : P ≠ 0) {r : σ → ℕ} (hr : ∀ j, 0 < r j) (α : σ → ℝ) :
    Roth.index P α r = 0 ↔ eval α P ≠ 0 := by
  rw [IndexConsumers133.targetBridge]
  have hs : (taylor α P).coeff.support.Nonempty := by simpa using hP
  rw [IndexEqZero139.nonnegativeZeroBridge (Roth.weight r) (taylor α P)
    (Roth.weight_nonneg r) hs]
  have hz (d : σ →₀ ℕ) :
      WithTop.some (NNReal.mk (Roth.weight r d) (Roth.weight_nonneg r d)) = ⊥ ↔ d = 0 := by
    change WithTop.some (NNReal.mk (Roth.weight r d) (Roth.weight_nonneg r d)) = 0 ↔ d = 0
    rw [WithTop.coe_eq_zero]
    exact (show NNReal.mk (Roth.weight r d) (Roth.weight_nonneg r d) = 0 ↔
      Roth.weight r d = 0 from Subtype.ext_iff).trans (Roth.weight_eq_zero_iff hr)
  have h := IndexEqZero139.abstractLaw
    (fun d ↦ WithTop.some (NNReal.mk (Roth.weight r d) (Roth.weight_nonneg r d)))
    (taylor α P) 0 hz
  change _ = ⊥ ↔ (taylor α P).coeff 0 ≠ 0 at h
  simpa only [coeff_taylor, hasseDeriv_zero_apply] using h
#print axioms boundedReplacement
end IndexEqZeroTarget139

namespace IndexShiftTarget140
open MvPolynomial
-- Generic finite-support shift law, reproduced in the actual project context.
noncomputable def minimum {σ R : Type*} [CommSemiring R]
    (D : (σ →₀ ℕ) → ℝ) (P : MvPolynomial σ R) : ℝ :=
  sInf (D '' (P.support : Set (σ →₀ ℕ)))
theorem supportShift {σ R : Type*} [CommSemiring R]
    (D : (σ →₀ ℕ) → ℝ) (P Q : MvPolynomial σ R) (k : σ →₀ ℕ)
    (hQ : Q ≠ 0) (hs : ∀ i, Q.coeff i ≠ 0 → P.coeff (i + k) ≠ 0)
    (hD : ∀ i, D (i + k) = D i + D k) :
    minimum D P - D k ≤ minimum D Q := by
  classical
  have hne : (D '' (Q.support : Set (σ →₀ ℕ))).Nonempty := by
    simpa using hQ
  obtain ⟨i, hi, himin⟩ := hne.csInf_mem (Q.support.finite_toSet.image D)
  have hp : minimum D P ≤ D (i + k) :=
    csInf_le (P.support.finite_toSet.image D).bddBelow
      (Set.mem_image_of_mem D (MvPolynomial.mem_support_iff.2 (hs i
        (MvPolynomial.mem_support_iff.1 hi))))
  apply sub_le_iff_le_add.mpr
  rw [hD i, himin] at hp
  exact hp
theorem pointShift {σ R : Type*} [CommRing R]
    (D : (σ →₀ ℕ) → ℝ) (P : MvPolynomial σ R) (α : σ → R) (k : σ →₀ ℕ)
    (hk : hasseDeriv k P ≠ 0) (hD : ∀ i, D (i + k) = D i + D k) :
    minimum D (taylor α P) - D k ≤ minimum D (taylor α (hasseDeriv k P)) := by
  apply supportShift D _ _ k (by simpa using hk) _ hD
  intro i hi
  rw [coeff_taylor] at hi ⊢
  rw [hasseDeriv_hasseDeriv, smul_eq_C_mul, map_mul, eval_C] at hi
  exact right_ne_zero_of_mul hi
theorem replacement {σ : Type*} [Fintype σ] {P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ} {k : σ →₀ ℕ}
    (hk : hasseDeriv k P ≠ 0) (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) :
    Roth.index P α r - Roth.weight r k ≤ Roth.index (hasseDeriv k P) α r :=
  pointShift (Roth.weight r) P α k hk (fun i ↦ Roth.weight_add r i k)
#print axioms supportShift
#print axioms pointShift
#print axioms replacement
end IndexShiftTarget140

namespace IndexShiftReaim140
open MvPolynomial
theorem projectedInfDegreeShift {σ R : Type*} [CommRing R]
    (D : (σ →₀ ℕ) → ℝ) (P : MvPolynomial σ R) (α : σ → R) (k : σ →₀ ℕ)
    (hk : hasseDeriv k P ≠ 0) (hD : ∀ i, D (i + k) = D i + D k) :
    (AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree (fun d ↦ (D d : WithTop ℝ))
      (taylor α P)).untopD 0 - D k ≤
    (AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree (fun d ↦ (D d : WithTop ℝ))
      (taylor α (hasseDeriv k P))).untopD 0 := by
  rw [show (AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree (fun d ↦ (D d : WithTop ℝ))
      (taylor α P)).untopD 0 = IndexShiftTarget140.minimum D (taylor α P) from
        IndexConsumers133.finsetBridge _ _,
    show (AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree (fun d ↦ (D d : WithTop ℝ))
      (taylor α (hasseDeriv k P))).untopD 0 =
        IndexShiftTarget140.minimum D (taylor α (hasseDeriv k P)) from
          IndexConsumers133.finsetBridge _ _]
  exact IndexShiftTarget140.pointShift D P α k hk hD
#print axioms projectedInfDegreeShift
end IndexShiftReaim140

namespace IndexShiftGuards140
open MvPolynomial Roth
theorem nonzeroHypothesisGuard :
    hasseDeriv (Finsupp.single true 1) (X false : MvPolynomial Bool ℝ) = 0 ∧
      ¬ (index (X false : MvPolynomial Bool ℝ) (fun _ ↦ 0)
          (fun b ↦ if b then 2 else 1) -
        weight (fun b : Bool ↦ if b then 2 else 1) (Finsupp.single true 1) ≤
        index (hasseDeriv (Finsupp.single true 1) (X false : MvPolynomial Bool ℝ))
          (fun _ ↦ 0) (fun b ↦ if b then 2 else 1)) := by
  have hz : hasseDeriv (Finsupp.single true 1) (X false : MvPolynomial Bool ℝ) = 0 :=
    hasseDeriv_eq_zero_of_degreeOf_lt (j := true) (by simp [degreeOf_X])
  refine ⟨hz, ?_⟩
  norm_num [hz, index, taylor_X, support_X, weight]
theorem allZeroDenominators {σ : Type*} [Fintype σ] {P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ}
    {k : σ →₀ ℕ} (hk : hasseDeriv k P ≠ 0) (α : σ → ℝ) :
    index P α (fun _ ↦ 0) - weight (fun _ ↦ 0) k ≤
      index (hasseDeriv k P) α (fun _ ↦ 0) :=
  IndexShiftTarget140.replacement hk α (fun _ ↦ 0)
#print axioms nonzeroHypothesisGuard

namespace IndexShiftReplay140
open Roth MvPolynomial
open scoped Pointwise
variable {τ : Type*} [Fintype τ]

theorem sum_max_sub_le_index_det {R : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℝ} {l : ℕ} (D : Fin l → (τ →₀ ℕ))
    (hD : ∀ μ, (D μ).degree ≤ μ) (hdet : (Matrix.of fun μ ν : Fin l ↦
      hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) R).det ≠ 0) (α : Option τ → ℝ)
    {r : Option τ → ℕ} (hr : ∀ j, 0 < r j) {δ : ℝ}
    (hδ : ∀ d : τ →₀ ℕ, d.degree ≤ l - 1 → weight (r ∘ some) d ≤ δ) :
    ∑ ν ∈ Finset.range l, max 0 (index R α r - ν / r none) - l * δ ≤
      index (Matrix.of fun μ ν : Fin l ↦
        hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) R).det α r := by
  classical
  set E : Fin l → Fin l → MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℝ := fun μ ν ↦
    hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) R
  have hterm : ∀ μ ν : Fin l, E μ ν ≠ 0 →
      max 0 (index R α r - ν / r none) - δ ≤ index (E μ ν) α r := by
    intro μ ν hE
    have h1 := IndexShiftTarget140.replacement hE α r
    rw [weight_add, weight_mapDomain (Option.some_injective _), weight_single] at h1
    have h2 := hδ (D μ) ((hD μ).trans (Nat.le_sub_one_of_lt μ.isLt))
    rw [← sub_sub, sub_right_comm] at h1
    rw [sub_le_iff_le_add]
    exact max_le (add_nonneg (index_nonneg _ _ _) ((weight_nonneg _ _).trans h2))
      ((sub_le_iff_le_add).1 ((sub_le_sub_left h2 _).trans h1))
  rw [Matrix.det_apply'] at hdet ⊢
  refine le_index_sum hdet fun s _ hs ↦ ?_
  have hprod : ∏ i, Matrix.of E (s i) i ≠ 0 := (mul_ne_zero_iff.mp hs).2
  have hentry (i : Fin l) : Matrix.of E (s i) i ≠ 0 :=
    (Finset.prod_ne_zero_iff.1 hprod) i (Finset.mem_univ _)
  have hidx : index (((Equiv.Perm.sign s : ℤ) : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℝ) *
      ∏ i, Matrix.of E (s i) i) α r = index (∏ i, Matrix.of E (s i) i) α r := by
    simpa only [map_intCast] using
      index_C_mul (Int.cast_ne_zero.2 (Units.ne_zero (Equiv.Perm.sign s)))
        (∏ i, Matrix.of E (s i) i) α r
  rw [hidx, index_prod _ (fun i _ ↦ hentry i) α hr,
    ← Fin.sum_univ_eq_sum_range (fun ν : ℕ ↦ max 0 (index R α r - ν / r none)) l]
  have hlδ : (l : ℝ) * δ = ∑ _i : Fin l, δ := by rw [Fin.sum_const, nsmul_eq_mul]
  rw [hlδ, ← Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
  exact Finset.sum_le_sum fun i _ ↦ hterm (s i) i (hentry i)


theorem exists_hasseDeriv_index_eval_bound_aux {σ : Type*} [Fintype σ] {P : MvPolynomial σ ℤ}
    {r : σ → ℕ} {α z : σ → ℝ} {C γ c : ℝ} (hP : P ≠ 0) (hd : ∀ j, degreeOf j P ≤ r j)
    (hbound : ∀ i k : σ →₀ ℕ,
      |eval α (map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) (hasseDeriv i (hasseDeriv k P)))| < C)
    (hγ : γ ≤ index (map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) P) α r)
    (hz : index (map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) P) z r < c) : ∃ Q : MvPolynomial σ ℤ, Q ≠ 0 ∧
      (∀ j, degreeOf j Q ≤ r j) ∧ γ - c < index (map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) Q) α r ∧
      eval z (map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) Q) ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ i : σ →₀ ℕ,
        |eval α (hasseDeriv i (map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) Q))| < C := by
  obtain ⟨k, hkw, hkev⟩ := exists_hasseDeriv_eval_ne_zero
    ((map_ne_zero_iff _ (MvPolynomial.map_injective _ Int.cast_injective)).2 hP) z r hz
  have hQev : eval z (map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) (hasseDeriv k P)) ≠ 0 := by
    simpa [hasseDeriv_map] using hkev
  refine ⟨hasseDeriv k P, fun h0 ↦ hQev (by rw [h0, map_zero, map_zero]),
    fun j ↦ (degreeOf_hasseDeriv_le j k P).trans (hd j), ?_, hQev, fun i ↦ ?_⟩
  · rw [← hasseDeriv_map]
    exact ((sub_lt_sub_left hkw γ).trans_le (sub_le_sub_right hγ _)).trans_le
      (IndexShiftTarget140.replacement (fun h0 ↦ hkev (by rw [h0, map_zero])) α r)
  · simpa [hasseDeriv_map] using hbound i k


#print axioms sum_max_sub_le_index_det
#print axioms exists_hasseDeriv_index_eval_bound_aux
end IndexShiftReplay140

theorem expandedProjectedShift {σ R : Type*} [CommRing R]
    (D : (σ →₀ ℕ) → ℝ) (P : MvPolynomial σ R) (α : σ → R) (k : σ →₀ ℕ)
    (hk : hasseDeriv k P ≠ 0) (hD : ∀ i, D (i + k) = D i + D k) :
    (AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree (fun d ↦ (D d : WithTop ℝ))
      (aeval (fun j ↦ X j + C (α j)) P)).untopD 0 - D k ≤
    (AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree (fun d ↦ (D d : WithTop ℝ))
      (aeval (fun j ↦ X j + C (α j)) (hasseDeriv k P))).untopD 0 :=
  IndexShiftReaim140.projectedInfDegreeShift D P α k hk hD

namespace IndexSum141
universe u v w z
open AddMonoidAlgebra
variable {R : Type u} {A : Type v} {T : Type w} {ι : Type z}
theorem finiteInfLaw [Semiring R] [SemilatticeInf T] [OrderTop T]
    (D : A → T) (s : Finset ι) (f : ι → AddMonoidAlgebra R A) :
    s.inf (fun i ↦ infDegree D (f i)) ≤ infDegree D (∑ i ∈ s, f i) :=
  AddMonoidAlgebra.supDegree_sum_le (B := OrderDual T)
theorem commonLowerBound [Semiring R] [SemilatticeInf T] [OrderTop T]
    (D : A → T) (s : Finset ι) (f : ι → AddMonoidAlgebra R A) (c : T)
    (hf : ∀ i ∈ s, c ≤ infDegree D (f i)) :
    c ≤ infDegree D (∑ i ∈ s, f i) :=
  (Finset.le_inf_iff.mpr hf).trans (finiteInfLaw D s f)
theorem projectedLaw [Semiring R] (D : A → ℝ) (s : Finset ι)
    (f : ι → AddMonoidAlgebra R A) (hs : ∑ i ∈ s, f i ≠ 0) (c : ℝ)
    (hf : ∀ i ∈ s, f i ≠ 0 →
      c ≤ (infDegree (fun a ↦ (D a : WithTop ℝ)) (f i)).untopD 0) :
    c ≤ (infDegree (fun a ↦ (D a : WithTop ℝ)) (∑ i ∈ s, f i)).untopD 0 := by
  classical
  have hh : (∑ i ∈ s, f i).coeff.support.Nonempty := by
    exact Finsupp.support_nonempty_iff.mpr
      (fun h0 ↦ hs (AddMonoidAlgebra.coeff_eq_zero.mp h0))
  have h := commonLowerBound (fun a ↦ (D a : WithTop ℝ)) s f (c : WithTop ℝ)
    (fun i hi ↦ by
      by_cases hfi : f i = 0
      · simp [hfi, infDegree]
      · exact (WithTop.coe_le_coe.mpr (hf i hi hfi)).trans (WithTop.coe_untopD_le _ 0))
  change c ≤ ((∑ i ∈ s, f i).coeff.support.inf (WithTop.some ∘ D)).untopD 0
  change (c : WithTop ℝ) ≤ (∑ i ∈ s, f i).coeff.support.inf (WithTop.some ∘ D) at h
  rw [← Finset.coe_inf' hh D] at h ⊢
  exact WithTop.coe_le_coe.mp h
#check @AddMonoidAlgebra.supDegree_sum_le
#check @AddMonoidAlgebra.le_infDegree_add
#check @Finset.le_inf_iff
#check @WithTop.coe_untopD_le
#print axioms finiteInfLaw
#print axioms commonLowerBound
#print axioms projectedLaw
end IndexSum141

namespace IndexSumTarget141
open MvPolynomial
universe u v w
theorem pointProjectedLaw {σ : Type u} {R : Type v} [CommRing R] {ι : Type w}
    (D : (σ →₀ ℕ) → ℝ) (s : Finset ι) (f : ι → MvPolynomial σ R) (α : σ → R)
    (hs : ∑ i ∈ s, f i ≠ 0) (c : ℝ)
    (hf : ∀ i ∈ s, f i ≠ 0 → c ≤
      (AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree (fun d ↦ (D d : WithTop ℝ))
        (aeval (fun j ↦ X j + C (α j)) (f i))).untopD 0) :
    c ≤ (AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree (fun d ↦ (D d : WithTop ℝ))
      (aeval (fun j ↦ X j + C (α j)) (∑ i ∈ s, f i))).untopD 0 := by
  rw [map_sum]
  refine IndexSum141.projectedLaw D s _ ?_ c ?_
  · simpa only [ne_eq, ← map_sum, ← taylor_apply, taylor_eq_zero_iff] using hs
  · intro i hi hfi
    exact hf i hi (fun h0 ↦ hfi (by rw [h0, map_zero]))
theorem replacement {σ : Type u} [Fintype σ] {ι : Type v} {s : Finset ι}
    {f : ι → MvPolynomial σ ℝ} {α : σ → ℝ} {r : σ → ℕ}
    (hs : ∑ i ∈ s, f i ≠ 0) {c : ℝ}
    (hf : ∀ i ∈ s, f i ≠ 0 → c ≤ Roth.index (f i) α r) :
    c ≤ Roth.index (∑ i ∈ s, f i) α r := by
  rw [IndexConsumers133.targetBridge, map_sum]
  refine IndexSum141.projectedLaw (Roth.weight r) s _ ?_ c ?_
  · simpa only [ne_eq, ← map_sum, taylor_eq_zero_iff] using hs
  · intro i hi hfi
    rw [← IndexConsumers133.targetBridge]
    exact hf i hi (fun h0 ↦ hfi (by rw [h0, map_zero]))
#print axioms pointProjectedLaw
#print axioms replacement
end IndexSumTarget141

namespace IndexSumGuards141
open Roth MvPolynomial
theorem emptySumNonzeroGuard : ¬ ((1 : ℝ) ≤
    index (∑ _i ∈ (∅ : Finset Unit), (X () : MvPolynomial Unit ℝ)) (fun _ ↦ 0) (fun _ ↦ 1)) := by
  simp [index]
theorem cancellationGuard :
    ∃ P : MvPolynomial Unit ℝ, P ≠ 0 ∧
      (0 < index P (fun _ ↦ 0) (fun _ ↦ 1)) ∧
      index (-P) (fun _ ↦ 0) (fun _ ↦ 1) = index P (fun _ ↦ 0) (fun _ ↦ 1) ∧
      index (P + -P) (fun _ ↦ 0) (fun _ ↦ 1) = 0 := by
  refine ⟨X (), X_ne_zero (), ?_, index_neg _ _ _, ?_⟩
  · apply lt_of_le_of_ne (index_nonneg _ _ _)
    intro h
    have hh := (index_eq_zero_iff (X_ne_zero ()) (fun _ ↦ by norm_num)
      (fun _ : Unit ↦ (0 : ℝ))).1 h.symm
    simp at hh
  · simp [index]
theorem allZeroDenominators {σ ι : Type*} [Fintype σ] {s : Finset ι}
    {f : ι → MvPolynomial σ ℝ} {α : σ → ℝ} (hs : ∑ i ∈ s, f i ≠ 0) {c : ℝ}
    (hf : ∀ i ∈ s, f i ≠ 0 → c ≤ index (f i) α (fun _ ↦ 0)) :
    c ≤ index (∑ i ∈ s, f i) α (fun _ ↦ 0) := IndexSumTarget141.replacement hs hf
#print axioms emptySumNonzeroGuard
#print axioms cancellationGuard
#print axioms allZeroDenominators
end IndexSumGuards141

namespace IndexSumReplay141
open Roth MvPolynomial
variable {τ : Type*} [Fintype τ]
theorem sum_max_sub_le_index_det {R : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℝ} {l : ℕ} (D : Fin l → (τ →₀ ℕ))
    (hD : ∀ μ, (D μ).degree ≤ μ) (hdet : (Matrix.of fun μ ν : Fin l ↦
      hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) R).det ≠ 0) (α : Option τ → ℝ)
    {r : Option τ → ℕ} (hr : ∀ j, 0 < r j) {δ : ℝ}
    (hδ : ∀ d : τ →₀ ℕ, d.degree ≤ l - 1 → weight (r ∘ some) d ≤ δ) :
    ∑ ν ∈ Finset.range l, max 0 (index R α r - ν / r none) - l * δ ≤
      index (Matrix.of fun μ ν : Fin l ↦
        hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) R).det α r := by
  classical
  set E : Fin l → Fin l → MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℝ := fun μ ν ↦
    hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) R
  have hterm : ∀ μ ν : Fin l, E μ ν ≠ 0 →
      max 0 (index R α r - ν / r none) - δ ≤ index (E μ ν) α r := by
    intro μ ν hE
    have h1 := index_sub_weight_le_index_hasseDeriv hE α r
    rw [weight_add, weight_mapDomain (Option.some_injective _), weight_single] at h1
    have h2 := hδ (D μ) ((hD μ).trans (Nat.le_sub_one_of_lt μ.isLt))
    rw [← sub_sub, sub_right_comm] at h1
    rw [sub_le_iff_le_add]
    exact max_le (add_nonneg (index_nonneg _ _ _) ((weight_nonneg _ _).trans h2))
      ((sub_le_iff_le_add).1 ((sub_le_sub_left h2 _).trans h1))
  rw [Matrix.det_apply'] at hdet ⊢
  refine IndexSumTarget141.replacement hdet fun s _ hs ↦ ?_
  have hprod : ∏ i, Matrix.of E (s i) i ≠ 0 := (mul_ne_zero_iff.mp hs).2
  have hentry (i : Fin l) : Matrix.of E (s i) i ≠ 0 :=
    (Finset.prod_ne_zero_iff.1 hprod) i (Finset.mem_univ _)
  have hidx : index (((Equiv.Perm.sign s : ℤ) : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℝ) *
      ∏ i, Matrix.of E (s i) i) α r = index (∏ i, Matrix.of E (s i) i) α r := by
    simpa only [map_intCast] using
      index_C_mul (Int.cast_ne_zero.2 (Units.ne_zero (Equiv.Perm.sign s)))
        (∏ i, Matrix.of E (s i) i) α r
  rw [hidx, index_prod _ (fun i _ ↦ hentry i) α hr,
    ← Fin.sum_univ_eq_sum_range (fun ν : ℕ ↦ max 0 (index R α r - ν / r none)) l]
  have hlδ : (l : ℝ) * δ = ∑ _i : Fin l, δ := by rw [Fin.sum_const, nsmul_eq_mul]
  rw [hlδ, ← Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
  exact Finset.sum_le_sum fun i _ ↦ hterm (s i) i (hentry i)


#print axioms sum_max_sub_le_index_det
end IndexSumReplay141

namespace WeightDiv142
theorem mapWeight {σ R M N : Type*} [Semiring R] [AddCommMonoid M] [AddCommMonoid N]
    [Module R M] [Module R N] (f : M →ₗ[R] N) (w : σ → M) (d : σ →₀ R) :
    f (Finsupp.weight w d) = Finsupp.weight (f ∘ w) d :=
  Finsupp.apply_linearCombination R f w d
variable {σ K : Type*} [Semifield K]
theorem castWeight (w : σ → ℕ) (d : σ →₀ ℕ) :
    (Finsupp.weight w d : K) = Finsupp.weight (fun i ↦ (w i : K)) d := by
  exact Finsupp.apply_linearCombination ℕ (Nat.castAddMonoidHom K).toNatLinearMap w d
theorem scaleWeight (w : σ → K) (c : K) (d : σ →₀ ℕ) :
    c * Finsupp.weight w d = Finsupp.weight (fun i ↦ c * w i) d := by
  exact Finsupp.apply_linearCombination ℕ
    ((DistribSMul.toLinearMap ℕ K c) : K →ₗ[ℕ] K) w d
variable [CharZero K] [Fintype σ]
theorem clear (r : σ → ℕ) (hr : ∀ i, r i ≠ 0) (d : σ →₀ ℕ) :
    Finsupp.weight (fun i ↦ (r i : K)⁻¹) d =
      (Finsupp.weight (fun j ↦ (∏ i, r i) / r j) d : K) / ∏ i, (r i : K) := by
  have hD : (∏ i, (r i : K)) ≠ 0 := by
    exact_mod_cast (Finset.prod_ne_zero_iff.mpr (fun i _ ↦ hr i))
  rw [castWeight, div_eq_inv_mul, scaleWeight]
  congr 2
  funext j
  rw [Nat.cast_div_charZero (Finset.dvd_prod_of_mem r (Finset.mem_univ j)), Nat.cast_prod,
    div_eq_mul_inv, ← mul_assoc, inv_mul_cancel₀ hD, one_mul]
end WeightDiv142

#check @Finsupp.apply_linearCombination
#check @DistribSMul.toLinearMap
#print axioms WeightDiv142.castWeight
#print axioms WeightDiv142.scaleWeight
#print axioms WeightDiv142.clear

#print axioms WeightDiv142.mapWeight

namespace WeightDivStrong142
variable {σ K : Type*} [Semifield K]
theorem supportClear (r : σ → ℕ) (d : σ →₀ ℕ) (D : ℕ) (hD : (D : K) ≠ 0)
    (hdiv : ∀ j ∈ d.support, r j ∣ D) :
    Finsupp.weight (fun j ↦ (r j : K)⁻¹) d =
      (Finsupp.weight (fun j ↦ D / r j) d : K) / D := by
  rw [WeightDiv142.castWeight, div_eq_inv_mul, WeightDiv142.scaleWeight]
  simp only [Finsupp.weight_apply, Finsupp.sum]
  apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
  intro j hj
  have hr : (r j : K) ≠ 0 := by
    intro h
    apply hD
    obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := hdiv j hj
    rw [hk, Nat.cast_mul, h, zero_mul]
  rw [Nat.cast_div (hdiv j hj) hr, div_eq_mul_inv, ← mul_assoc,
    inv_mul_cancel₀ hD, one_mul]
end WeightDivStrong142
#print axioms WeightDivStrong142.supportClear

namespace WeightDivTarget142
open MvPolynomial
variable {σ : Type*} [Fintype σ]
theorem replacement {r : σ → ℕ} (hr : ∀ j, 0 < r j) (d : σ →₀ ℕ) :
    Roth.weight r d = (Finsupp.weight (fun j ↦ (∏ i, r i) / r j) d : ℝ) / ∏ i, r i := by
  rw [← IndexExpanded133.weightBridge]
  simpa only [Nat.cast_prod] using
    (WeightDiv142.clear (K := ℝ) r (fun j ↦ (hr j).ne') d)
theorem caller {P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ} (hP : P ≠ 0) {r : σ → ℕ}
    (hr : ∀ j, 0 < r j) (α : σ → ℝ) : Roth.index P α r =
      (((taylor α P : MvPowerSeries σ ℝ).weightedOrder fun j ↦ (∏ i, r i) / r j).toNat : ℝ) /
        ∏ i, r i := by
  set w : σ → ℕ := fun j ↦ (∏ i, r i) / r j
  set F : MvPowerSeries σ ℝ := ↑(taylor α P)
  obtain ⟨d, hd, hdw⟩ := MvPowerSeries.exists_coeff_ne_zero_and_weightedOrder w (f := F)
    ((MvPowerSeries.ne_zero_iff_weightedOrder_finite w).1 (by simpa [F] using hP))
  apply le_antisymm
  · rw [← hdw, ENat.toNat_natCast, ← replacement hr]
    exact Roth.index_le_weight (by simpa [F, MvPolynomial.coeff_coe, coeff_taylor] using hd)
  · refine (Roth.le_index_iff hP).2 fun e he ↦ ?_
    rw [replacement hr]
    refine div_le_div_of_nonneg_right ?_ (Nat.cast_nonneg _)
    exact_mod_cast ENat.toNat_le_toNat (MvPowerSeries.weightedOrder_le w (f := F) (d := e)
      (by simpa [F, MvPolynomial.coeff_coe, coeff_taylor] using he)) (by simp)
end WeightDivTarget142

namespace IndexWeightedOrderTarget143
open MvPolynomial
variable {σ : Type*} [Fintype σ]
open Roth
theorem replacementNonzero {P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ} (hP : P ≠ 0) {r : σ → ℕ}
    (hr : ∀ j, 0 < r j) (α : σ → ℝ) : index P α r =
      (((taylor α P : MvPowerSeries σ ℝ).weightedOrder fun j ↦ (∏ i, r i) / r j).toNat : ℝ) /
        ∏ i, r i := by
  set w : σ → ℕ := fun j ↦ (∏ i, r i) / r j
  set F : MvPowerSeries σ ℝ := ↑(taylor α P)
  obtain ⟨d, hd, hdw⟩ := MvPowerSeries.exists_coeff_ne_zero_and_weightedOrder w (f := F)
    ((MvPowerSeries.ne_zero_iff_weightedOrder_finite w).1 (by simpa [F] using hP))
  apply le_antisymm
  · rw [← hdw, ENat.toNat_natCast, ← weight_eq_finsuppWeight_div hr]
    exact index_le_weight (by simpa [F, MvPolynomial.coeff_coe, coeff_taylor] using hd)
  · refine (le_index_iff hP).2 fun e he ↦ ?_
    rw [weight_eq_finsuppWeight_div hr]
    refine div_le_div_of_nonneg_right ?_ (Nat.cast_nonneg _)
    exact_mod_cast ENat.toNat_le_toNat (MvPowerSeries.weightedOrder_le w (f := F) (d := e)
      (by simpa [F, MvPolynomial.coeff_coe, coeff_taylor] using he)) (by simp)

theorem replacement (P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ) {r : σ → ℕ}
    (hr : ∀ j, 0 < r j) (α : σ → ℝ) : index P α r =
      (((taylor α P : MvPowerSeries σ ℝ).weightedOrder fun j ↦ (∏ i, r i) / r j).toNat : ℝ) /
        ∏ i, r i := by
  by_cases hP : P = 0
  · subst P
    simp [index]
  · exact replacementNonzero hP hr α
theorem fullCaller {P Q : MvPolynomial σ ℝ} (hP : P ≠ 0) (hQ : Q ≠ 0) (α : σ → ℝ) {r : σ → ℕ}
    (hr : ∀ j, 0 < r j) : index (P * Q) α r = index P α r + index Q α r := by
  rw [replacement P hr, replacement Q hr,
    replacement (P * Q) hr, ← add_div, map_mul, MvPolynomial.coe_mul,
    MvPowerSeries.weightedOrder_mul]
  congr 1
  have hfin : ∀ R : MvPolynomial σ ℝ, R ≠ 0 →
      ((taylor α R : MvPowerSeries σ ℝ).weightedOrder fun j ↦ (∏ i, r i) / r j) ≠ ⊤ := by simp
  rw [ENat.toNat_add (hfin P hP) (hfin Q hQ), Nat.cast_add]

#print axioms replacementNonzero

namespace IndexWeightedOrder143
open MvPolynomial
universe u v
theorem weightedOrder_coe {σ : Type u} {R : Type v} [CommSemiring R]
    (P : MvPolynomial σ R) (w : σ → ℕ) :
    (P : MvPowerSeries σ R).weightedOrder w =
      AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree (fun d ↦ (Finsupp.weight w d : ℕ∞)) P := by
  classical
  apply le_antisymm
  · apply Finset.le_inf_iff.2
    intro d hd
    exact MvPowerSeries.weightedOrder_le w (by simpa using mem_support_iff.1 hd)
  · apply MvPowerSeries.le_weightedOrder
    intro d hd
    by_contra hn
    have hm : d ∈ P.support := mem_support_iff.2 (by simpa using hn)
    exact (not_lt_of_ge (Finset.inf_le (f := fun e ↦ (Finsupp.weight w e : ℕ∞)) hm)) hd
#print axioms weightedOrder_coe
end IndexWeightedOrder143

namespace IndexWeightedScale143
open MvPolynomial
universe u v
theorem scaledMinimum {σ : Type u} {R : Type v} [CommSemiring R]
    (P : MvPolynomial σ R) (w : σ → ℕ) (D : ℝ) (hD : 0 ≤ D) :
    sInf ((fun d ↦ (Finsupp.weight w d : ℝ) / D) '' (P.support : Set (σ →₀ ℕ))) =
      (((P : MvPowerSeries σ R).weightedOrder w).toNat : ℝ) / D := by
  classical
  by_cases hP : P = 0
  · subst P
    simp
  obtain ⟨d, hd, hdw⟩ := MvPowerSeries.exists_coeff_ne_zero_and_weightedOrder w
    (f := (P : MvPowerSeries σ R))
    ((MvPowerSeries.ne_zero_iff_weightedOrder_finite w).1 (by simpa using hP))
  have hmem : d ∈ P.support := mem_support_iff.2 (by simpa using hd)
  apply le_antisymm
  · rw [← hdw, ENat.toNat_natCast]
    exact csInf_le (P.support.finite_toSet.image _).bddBelow (Set.mem_image_of_mem _ hmem)
  · refine le_csInf (by simpa using hP) ?_
    rintro _ ⟨e, he, rfl⟩
    apply div_le_div_of_nonneg_right _ hD
    exact_mod_cast ENat.toNat_le_toNat (MvPowerSeries.weightedOrder_le w
      (f := (P : MvPowerSeries σ R)) (by simpa using mem_support_iff.1 he)) (by simp)
theorem finiteProjection {ι : Type u} (s : Finset ι) (v : ι → ℝ) :
    (s.inf (fun d ↦ (v d : WithTop ℝ))).untopD 0 = sInf (v '' (s : Set ι)) := by
  classical
  rcases s.eq_empty_or_nonempty with h | h
  · subst s
    simp
  · change (s.inf (WithTop.some ∘ v)).untopD 0 = _
    rw [← Finset.coe_inf' h v, Finset.inf'_eq_csInf_image]
    rfl
theorem scaledInfDegree {σ : Type u} {R : Type v} [CommSemiring R]
    (P : MvPolynomial σ R) (w : σ → ℕ) (D : ℝ) (hD : 0 ≤ D) :
    (AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree
      (fun d ↦ (((Finsupp.weight w d : ℝ) / D) : WithTop ℝ)) P).untopD 0 =
      (((P : MvPowerSeries σ R).weightedOrder w).toNat : ℝ) / D :=
  (finiteProjection _ _).trans (scaledMinimum P w D hD)
#print axioms scaledMinimum
#print axioms finiteProjection
#print axioms scaledInfDegree
end IndexWeightedScale143

namespace IndexMul144
open MvPolynomial Roth
variable {σ : Type*} [Fintype σ]
theorem clearedWeight (r : σ → ℕ) (d : σ →₀ ℕ) :
    weight r d = (Finsupp.weight (fun j ↦ (∏ i, max 1 (r i)) / r j) d : ℝ) /
      ((∏ i, max 1 (r i) : ℕ) : ℝ) := by
  classical
  have hD : (0 : ℝ) < ((∏ i, max 1 (r i) : ℕ) : ℝ) := by
    exact_mod_cast Finset.prod_pos (fun i _ ↦ lt_of_lt_of_le Nat.zero_lt_one (le_max_left _ _))
  rw [weight, Finsupp.weight_eq_sum, Nat.cast_sum, Finset.sum_div]
  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j _ ↦ ?_
  by_cases hj : r j = 0
  · simp [hj]
  · have hdiv : r j ∣ ∏ i, max 1 (r i) := by
      have hm : max 1 (r j) = r j := max_eq_right (Nat.one_le_iff_ne_zero.2 hj)
      rw [← hm]
      exact Finset.dvd_prod_of_mem _ (Finset.mem_univ j)
    rw [smul_eq_mul, Nat.cast_mul, Nat.cast_div_charZero hdiv,
      ← mul_div_assoc, div_right_comm, mul_div_cancel_right₀ _ hD.ne']
theorem comparison (P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ) (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) :
    index P α r =
      (((taylor α P : MvPowerSeries σ ℝ).weightedOrder
        (fun j ↦ (∏ i, max 1 (r i)) / r j)).toNat : ℝ) /
        ((∏ i, max 1 (r i) : ℕ) : ℝ) := by
  rw [IndexConsumers133.targetBridge]
  have hw : (fun d ↦ (weight r d : WithTop ℝ)) =
      (fun d ↦ (((Finsupp.weight (fun j ↦ (∏ i, max 1 (r i)) / r j) d : ℝ) /
        ((∏ i, max 1 (r i) : ℕ) : ℝ)) : WithTop ℝ)) := by
    funext d
    rw [clearedWeight]
  rw [hw]
  exact IndexWeightedScale143.scaledInfDegree (taylor α P)
    (fun j ↦ (∏ i, max 1 (r i)) / r j) _ (Nat.cast_nonneg _)
theorem replacement {P Q : MvPolynomial σ ℝ} (hP : P ≠ 0) (hQ : Q ≠ 0)
    (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) : index (P * Q) α r = index P α r + index Q α r := by
  rw [comparison, comparison, comparison, ← add_div, map_mul, MvPolynomial.coe_mul,
    MvPowerSeries.weightedOrder_mul]
  congr 1
  have hfin : ∀ R : MvPolynomial σ ℝ, R ≠ 0 →
      ((taylor α R : MvPowerSeries σ ℝ).weightedOrder
        (fun j ↦ (∏ i, max 1 (r i)) / r j)) ≠ ⊤ := by simp
  rw [ENat.toNat_add (hfin P hP) (hfin Q hQ), Nat.cast_add]
theorem zeroLeftGuard :
    index (0 * (X () : MvPolynomial Unit ℝ)) (fun _ ↦ 0) (fun _ ↦ 1) ≠
      index (0 : MvPolynomial Unit ℝ) (fun _ ↦ 0) (fun _ ↦ 1) +
        index (X () : MvPolynomial Unit ℝ) (fun _ ↦ 0) (fun _ ↦ 1) := by
  simp [index, taylor_X, support_X, weight]
theorem zeroRightGuard :
    index ((X () : MvPolynomial Unit ℝ) * 0) (fun _ ↦ 0) (fun _ ↦ 1) ≠
      index (X () : MvPolynomial Unit ℝ) (fun _ ↦ 0) (fun _ ↦ 1) +
        index (0 : MvPolynomial Unit ℝ) (fun _ ↦ 0) (fun _ ↦ 1) := by
  simp [index, taylor_X, support_X, weight]
#print axioms clearedWeight
#print axioms comparison
#print axioms replacement
#print axioms zeroLeftGuard
#print axioms zeroRightGuard
end IndexMul144

namespace IndexMulCallers144
open Roth MvPolynomial
variable {σ : Type*} [Fintype σ]
theorem index_prod {ι : Type*} (s : Finset ι) {f : ι → MvPolynomial σ ℝ} (hf : ∀ i ∈ s, f i ≠ 0)
    (α : σ → ℝ) {r : σ → ℕ} (hr : ∀ j, 0 < r j) :
    index (∏ i ∈ s, f i) α r = ∑ i ∈ s, index (f i) α r := by
  classical
  induction s using Finset.induction_on with
  | empty =>
    rw [Finset.prod_empty, Finset.sum_empty]
    exact (index_eq_zero_iff one_ne_zero hr α).2 (by simp)
  | insert a s has ih =>
    rw [Finset.prod_insert has, Finset.sum_insert has,
      IndexMul144.replacement (hf a (Finset.mem_insert_self a s))
        (Finset.prod_ne_zero_iff.2 fun i hi ↦ hf i (Finset.mem_insert_of_mem hi)) α r,
      ih fun i hi ↦ hf i (Finset.mem_insert_of_mem hi)]
variable {τ : Type*} [Fintype τ]
theorem index_separated_mul_le_aux {r : Option τ → ℕ} (_hr0 : ∀ j, 0 < r j) {q : Option τ → ℕ}
    {h : Option τ → ℤ} (hh : ∀ j, IsCoprime (h j) (q j : ℤ)) {M : ℝ} {l : ℕ} (hl : 0 < l)
    {U : MvPolynomial τ ℤ} (hU : U ∈ RSet M (fun j ↦ l * r (some j))) {V : Polynomial ℤ}
    (hV : V ≠ 0) (hVd : V.natDegree ≤ l * r none) (hVc : ∀ b, |(V.coeff b : ℝ)| ≤ M) :
    index (map (Int.castRingHom ℝ)
        (rename some U * Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℤ) V))
      (fun j ↦ (h j : ℝ) / q j) r ≤ l * lem6Φ q r M l := by
  classical
  set V' : MvPolynomial Unit ℤ := (uniqueAlgEquiv ℤ Unit).symm V with hV'
  have hV'0 : V' ≠ 0 := by
    rwa [hV', ne_eq, map_eq_zero_iff _ (uniqueAlgEquiv ℤ Unit).symm.injective]
  have hVmem : V' ∈ RSet M (fun _ : Unit ↦ l * r none) := by
    refine ⟨hV'0, fun j ↦ ?_, fun d ↦ ?_⟩
    · rw [Subsingleton.elim j default, hV', degreeOf_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm]
      exact hVd
    · rw [hV', MvPolynomial.coeff_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm]
      exact hVc _
  have hinjV : Function.Injective (fun _ : Unit ↦ (none : Option τ)) :=
    fun a b _ ↦ Subsingleton.elim a b
  have hmU : map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) U ≠ 0 := fun h0 ↦
    hU.1 (map_injective _ Int.cast_injective (h0.trans (map_zero _).symm))
  have hmV : map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) V' ≠ 0 := fun h0 ↦
    hV'0 (map_injective _ Int.cast_injective (h0.trans (map_zero _).symm))
  rw [aeval_X_eq_rename, _root_.map_mul, map_rename, map_rename,
    IndexMul144.replacement ((map_ne_zero_iff (rename (R := ℝ) some)
      (rename_injective _ (Option.some_injective _))).2 hmU)
      ((map_ne_zero_iff (rename (R := ℝ) (fun _ : Unit ↦ (none : Option τ)))
        (rename_injective _ hinjV)).2 hmV) _ r,
    index_rename (Option.some_injective _), index_rename hinjV]
  have hUΘ := index_le_Θ hU (h := h ∘ some)
    fun j ↦ hh (some j)
  have hVΘ := index_le_Θ hVmem
    (q := fun _ : Unit ↦ q none) (h := fun _ ↦ h none) fun _ ↦ hh none
  rw [index_mul_left_weights] at hUΘ hVΘ
  have hlR : (0 : ℝ) < l := by exact_mod_cast hl
  rw [lem6Φ, mul_add, add_comm]
  exact add_le_add ((div_le_iff₀' hlR).1 hVΘ) ((div_le_iff₀' hlR).1 hUΘ)
#print axioms index_prod
#print axioms index_separated_mul_le_aux
end IndexMulCallers144

namespace IndexMulComposition144
open Roth MvPolynomial
variable {σ : Type*} [Fintype σ]
theorem exactAttempt {P Q : MvPolynomial σ ℝ} (hP : P ≠ 0) (hQ : Q ≠ 0)
    (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) : index (P * Q) α r = index P α r + index Q α r := by
  fail_if_success
    exact MvPowerSeries.weightedOrder_mul
      (fun j ↦ (∏ i, max 1 (r i)) / r j)
      (taylor α P : MvPowerSeries σ ℝ) (taylor α Q : MvPowerSeries σ ℝ)
  fail_if_success (solve | simp only [index, map_mul])
  exact IndexMul144.replacement hP hQ α r
#print axioms exactAttempt
end IndexMulComposition144

namespace IndexProd145
open MvPolynomial Roth
variable {σ : Type*} [Fintype σ]
theorem one (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) : index (1 : MvPolynomial σ ℝ) α r = 0 := by
  simp [index, weight]
theorem replacement {ι : Type*} (s : Finset ι) {f : ι → MvPolynomial σ ℝ}
    (hf : ∀ i ∈ s, f i ≠ 0) (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) :
    index (∏ i ∈ s, f i) α r = ∑ i ∈ s, index (f i) α r := by
  classical
  induction s using Finset.induction_on with
  | empty => simpa using one α r
  | insert a s has ih =>
    rw [Finset.prod_insert has, Finset.sum_insert has,
      IndexMul144.replacement (hf a (Finset.mem_insert_self a s))
        (Finset.prod_ne_zero_iff.2 fun i hi ↦ hf i (Finset.mem_insert_of_mem hi)) α r,
      ih fun i hi ↦ hf i (Finset.mem_insert_of_mem hi)]
theorem nonzeroGuard :
    index (∏ i ∈ ({false, true} : Finset Bool),
      if i then (X () : MvPolynomial Unit ℝ) else 0) (fun _ ↦ 0) (fun _ ↦ 1) ≠
      ∑ i ∈ ({false, true} : Finset Bool),
        index (if i then (X () : MvPolynomial Unit ℝ) else 0) (fun _ ↦ 0) (fun _ ↦ 1) := by
  simp [index, support_X, weight]
#print axioms one
#print axioms replacement
#print axioms nonzeroGuard
end IndexProd145

namespace IndexProdCallers145
open MvPolynomial Roth
open scoped Pointwise
variable {τ : Type*} [Fintype τ]
theorem sum_max_sub_le_index_det {R : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℝ} {l : ℕ} (D : Fin l → (τ →₀ ℕ))
    (hD : ∀ μ, (D μ).degree ≤ μ) (hdet : (Matrix.of fun μ ν : Fin l ↦
      hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) R).det ≠ 0) (α : Option τ → ℝ)
    {r : Option τ → ℕ} (_hr : ∀ j, 0 < r j) {δ : ℝ}
    (hδ : ∀ d : τ →₀ ℕ, d.degree ≤ l - 1 → weight (r ∘ some) d ≤ δ) :
    ∑ ν ∈ Finset.range l, max 0 (index R α r - ν / r none) - l * δ ≤
      index (Matrix.of fun μ ν : Fin l ↦
        hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) R).det α r := by
  classical
  set E : Fin l → Fin l → MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℝ := fun μ ν ↦
    hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) R
  have hterm : ∀ μ ν : Fin l, E μ ν ≠ 0 →
      max 0 (index R α r - ν / r none) - δ ≤ index (E μ ν) α r := by
    intro μ ν hE
    have h1 := index_sub_weight_le_index_hasseDeriv hE α r
    rw [weight_add, weight_mapDomain (Option.some_injective _), weight_single] at h1
    have h2 := hδ (D μ) ((hD μ).trans (Nat.le_sub_one_of_lt μ.isLt))
    rw [← sub_sub, sub_right_comm] at h1
    rw [sub_le_iff_le_add]
    exact max_le (add_nonneg (index_nonneg _ _ _) ((weight_nonneg _ _).trans h2))
      ((sub_le_iff_le_add).1 ((sub_le_sub_left h2 _).trans h1))
  rw [Matrix.det_apply'] at hdet ⊢
  refine le_index_sum hdet fun s _ hs ↦ ?_
  have hprod : ∏ i, Matrix.of E (s i) i ≠ 0 := (mul_ne_zero_iff.mp hs).2
  have hentry (i : Fin l) : Matrix.of E (s i) i ≠ 0 :=
    (Finset.prod_ne_zero_iff.1 hprod) i (Finset.mem_univ _)
  have hidx : index (((Equiv.Perm.sign s : ℤ) : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℝ) *
      ∏ i, Matrix.of E (s i) i) α r = index (∏ i, Matrix.of E (s i) i) α r := by
    simpa only [map_intCast] using
      index_C_mul (Int.cast_ne_zero.2 (Units.ne_zero (Equiv.Perm.sign s)))
        (∏ i, Matrix.of E (s i) i) α r
  rw [hidx, IndexProd145.replacement _ (fun i _ ↦ hentry i) α r,
    ← Fin.sum_univ_eq_sum_range (fun ν : ℕ ↦ max 0 (index R α r - ν / r none)) l]
  have hlδ : (l : ℝ) * δ = ∑ _i : Fin l, δ := by rw [Fin.sum_const, nsmul_eq_mul]
  rw [hlδ, ← Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
  exact Finset.sum_le_sum fun i _ ↦ hterm (s i) i (hentry i)
#print axioms sum_max_sub_le_index_det
end IndexProdCallers145

namespace IndexProdComposition145
open MvPolynomial Roth
variable {σ : Type*} [Fintype σ]
theorem exactAttempt {ι : Type*} (s : Finset ι) {f : ι → MvPolynomial σ ℝ}
    (hf : ∀ i ∈ s, f i ≠ 0) (α : σ → ℝ) {r : σ → ℕ} (_hr : ∀ j, 0 < r j) :
    index (∏ i ∈ s, f i) α r = ∑ i ∈ s, index (f i) α r := by
  fail_if_success
    exact MvPowerSeries.weightedOrder_prod
      (fun j ↦ (∏ i, r i) / r j) (fun i ↦ (taylor α (f i) : MvPowerSeries σ ℝ)) s
  fail_if_success (solve | simp only [index, map_prod])
  exact IndexProd145.replacement s hf α r
#print axioms exactAttempt

namespace IndexNeg146
open MvPolynomial Roth
variable {σ : Type*} [Fintype σ]
theorem replacement (P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ) (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) :
    index (-P) α r = index P α r := by
  rw [IndexConsumers133.targetBridge, IndexConsumers133.targetBridge, map_neg]
  exact congrArg (WithTop.untopD 0)
    (AddMonoidAlgebra.supDegree_neg (B := OrderDual (WithTop ℝ)))
theorem originalComposition (P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ) (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) :
    index (-P) α r = index P α r := by
  simp only [index, map_neg, support_neg]
theorem zero (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) : index (-(0 : MvPolynomial σ ℝ)) α r = 0 := by
  simp [index]
#print axioms replacement
#print axioms originalComposition
#print axioms zero
end IndexNeg146

namespace IndexCMulCaller147
open MvPolynomial Roth
variable {τ : Type*} [Fintype τ]
theorem callerReplay {R : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℝ} {l : ℕ} (D : Fin l → (τ →₀ ℕ))
    (hD : ∀ μ, (D μ).degree ≤ μ) (hdet : (Matrix.of fun μ ν : Fin l ↦
      hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) R).det ≠ 0) (α : Option τ → ℝ)
    {r : Option τ → ℕ} (hr : ∀ j, 0 < r j) {δ : ℝ}
    (hδ : ∀ d : τ →₀ ℕ, d.degree ≤ l - 1 → weight (r ∘ some) d ≤ δ) :
    ∑ ν ∈ Finset.range l, max 0 (index R α r - ν / r none) - l * δ ≤
      index (Matrix.of fun μ ν : Fin l ↦
        hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) R).det α r := by
  classical
  set E : Fin l → Fin l → MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℝ := fun μ ν ↦
    hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) R
  have hterm : ∀ μ ν : Fin l, E μ ν ≠ 0 →
      max 0 (index R α r - ν / r none) - δ ≤ index (E μ ν) α r := by
    intro μ ν hE
    have h1 := index_sub_weight_le_index_hasseDeriv hE α r
    rw [weight_add, weight_mapDomain (Option.some_injective _), weight_single] at h1
    have h2 := hδ (D μ) ((hD μ).trans (Nat.le_sub_one_of_lt μ.isLt))
    rw [← sub_sub, sub_right_comm] at h1
    rw [sub_le_iff_le_add]
    exact max_le (add_nonneg (index_nonneg _ _ _) ((weight_nonneg _ _).trans h2))
      ((sub_le_iff_le_add).1 ((sub_le_sub_left h2 _).trans h1))
  rw [Matrix.det_apply'] at hdet ⊢
  refine le_index_sum hdet fun s _ hs ↦ ?_
  have hprod : ∏ i, Matrix.of E (s i) i ≠ 0 := (mul_ne_zero_iff.mp hs).2
  have hentry (i : Fin l) : Matrix.of E (s i) i ≠ 0 :=
    (Finset.prod_ne_zero_iff.1 hprod) i (Finset.mem_univ _)
  have hidx : index (((Equiv.Perm.sign s : ℤ) : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℝ) *
      ∏ i, Matrix.of E (s i) i) α r = index (∏ i, Matrix.of E (s i) i) α r := by
    simpa only [map_intCast] using
      IndexCMul147.replacement (Int.cast_ne_zero.2 (Units.ne_zero (Equiv.Perm.sign s)))
        (∏ i, Matrix.of E (s i) i) α r
  rw [hidx, index_prod _ (fun i _ ↦ hentry i) α hr,
    ← Fin.sum_univ_eq_sum_range (fun ν : ℕ ↦ max 0 (index R α r - ν / r none)) l]
  have hlδ : (l : ℝ) * δ = ∑ _i : Fin l, δ := by rw [Fin.sum_const, nsmul_eq_mul]
  rw [hlδ, ← Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
  exact Finset.sum_le_sum fun i _ ↦ hterm (s i) i (hentry i)

#print axioms callerReplay
end IndexCMulCaller147

namespace IndexCMulBridge147
open MvPolynomial Roth
variable {σ : Type*} [Fintype σ]
theorem replacement {c : ℝ} (hc : c ≠ 0) (P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ)
    (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) : index (C c * P) α r = index P α r := by
  classical
  rw [IndexConsumers133.targetBridge, IndexConsumers133.targetBridge,
    map_mul, taylor_C, ← smul_eq_C_mul]
  exact congrArg (WithTop.untopD 0)
    (congrArg (fun s : Finset (σ →₀ ℕ) ↦ s.inf (fun d ↦ (weight r d : WithTop ℝ)))
      (Finsupp.support_smul_eq hc (g := (taylor α P).coeff)))

    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.MainLemma `Roth.exists_auxiliary_polynomial_with_index_gap_aux,
    `Roth.main_lemma,
    `Roth.approxSet,
    `Roth.exists_nat_mul_isIntegral,
    `Roth.approxSet_den_le_finite,
    `Roth.eventually_div_rpow_lt,
    `Roth.reduction,
    `Roth.exists_monic_aeval_eq_zero,

    `Roth.abs_eval_lt_of_le_index,

    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.exists_roth_approximants_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.one_lt_roth_eval_bound_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.roth_product_bound_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.roth_log_bound_aux,
    `Roth.roth_of_isIntegral,
    `Roth.roth,
    `Roth.roth_rat,
    `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.IsDiophantine,

      `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.exists_pos_div_rpow_lt_aux,
    `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.isDiophantine_iff,
    `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.thueSiegelRoth]
  let unique := names.toList.eraseDups.length
  unless names.size == 293 && unique == 293 do
    throwError "INDEX_SCALE148_BAD_CANONICAL_ARRAY"
  let mut present : Nat := 0
  let mut values : Nat := 0
  for n in names do
    match env.find? n with
    | none => throwError "INDEX_SCALE148_NOT_LOADED: {n}"
    | some ci =>
      present := present + 1
      match ci.value? (allowOpaque := true) with
      | none => pure ()
      | some val =>
        values := values + 1
        if val.getUsedConstants.any (fun c ↦ c == target) then
          Lean.logInfo m!"INDEX_SCALE148_DIRECT_CONSUMER: {n}"
  Lean.logInfo m!"INDEX_SCALE148_AUDIT: requested={names.size}; unique={unique}"
  Lean.logInfo m!"INDEX_SCALE148_AUDIT_PRESENT: present={present}; values={values}"
end IndexScaleAudit148

namespace IndexScaleMathlib148
universe u v w z
theorem transport {A : Type u} {R : Type v} {T : Type w} {U : Type z}
    [Semiring R] [SemilatticeInf T] [OrderTop T] [SemilatticeInf U] [OrderTop U]
    (h : InfTopHom T U) (D : A → T) (f : AddMonoidAlgebra R A) :
    h (AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree D f) = AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree (h ∘ D) f :=
  map_finset_inf h f.coeff.support D
def liftMonotone {K : Type w} {L : Type z} [LinearOrder K] [LinearOrder L]
    (φ : K → L) (hφ : Monotone φ) : InfTopHom (WithTop K) (WithTop L) where
  toFun := WithTop.map φ
  map_top' := rfl
  map_inf' a b := hφ.withTop_map.map_min (a := a) (b := b)
theorem projection {K : Type w} {L : Type z} [Zero K] [Zero L]
    (φ : K → L) (h0 : φ 0 = 0) (x : WithTop K) :
    (WithTop.map φ x).untopD 0 = φ (x.untopD 0) := by
  cases x <;> simp [h0]
theorem projectedTransport {A : Type u} {R : Type v} {K : Type w} {L : Type z}
    [Semiring R] [LinearOrder K] [Zero K] [LinearOrder L] [Zero L]
    (φ : K → L) (hφ : Monotone φ) (h0 : φ 0 = 0) (D : A → K)
    (f : AddMonoidAlgebra R A) :
    (AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree (fun a ↦ (φ (D a) : WithTop L)) f).untopD 0 =
      φ ((AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree (fun a ↦ (D a : WithTop K)) f).untopD 0) := by
  have h := congrArg (WithTop.untopD 0)
    (transport (liftMonotone φ hφ) (fun a ↦ (D a : WithTop K)) f)
  exact h.symm.trans (projection φ h0 _)
theorem scale {A : Type u} {R : Type v} [Semiring R] (c : ℝ) (hc : 0 ≤ c)
    (D : A → ℝ) (f : AddMonoidAlgebra R A) :
    (AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree (fun a ↦ (c * D a : WithTop ℝ)) f).untopD 0 =
      c * (AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree (fun a ↦ (D a : WithTop ℝ)) f).untopD 0 :=
  projectedTransport (c * ·) (fun _ _ h ↦ mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h hc) (mul_zero c) D f
#print axioms transport
#print axioms liftMonotone
#print axioms projection
#print axioms projectedTransport
#print axioms scale
end IndexScaleMathlib148

namespace IndexScale148
open MvPolynomial Roth
open scoped Pointwise
variable {σ : Type*} [Fintype σ]
theorem replacement {l : ℕ} (P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ) (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) :
    index P α (fun j ↦ l * r j) = index P α r / l := by
  have himg : weight (fun j ↦ l * r j) '' ((taylor α P).support : Set (σ →₀ ℕ)) =
      (l : ℝ)⁻¹ • (weight r '' ((taylor α P).support : Set (σ →₀ ℕ))) := by
    rw [← Set.image_smul, Set.image_image]
    refine Set.image_congr fun d _ ↦ ?_
    rw [weight_mul_left, smul_eq_mul, div_eq_inv_mul]
  rw [index, index, himg, Real.sInf_smul_of_nonneg (by positivity), smul_eq_mul, div_eq_inv_mul]
theorem zeroScale (P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ) (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) :
    index P α (fun j ↦ 0 * r j) = 0 := by
  rw [replacement, Nat.cast_zero, div_zero]
theorem zeroPolynomial (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) (l : ℕ) :
    index (0 : MvPolynomial σ ℝ) α (fun j ↦ l * r j) = 0 := by
  simp [index]
theorem noAutomaticUnfold {l : ℕ} (P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ) (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) :
    index P α (fun j ↦ l * r j) = index P α r / l := by
  fail_if_success rfl
  exact replacement P α r
#print axioms replacement
#print axioms zeroScale
#print axioms zeroPolynomial
#print axioms noAutomaticUnfold
end IndexScale148

namespace IndexScaleCaller148
open MvPolynomial Roth
variable {τ : Type*} [Fintype τ]
theorem callerReplay {r : Option τ → ℕ} (hr0 : ∀ j, 0 < r j) {q : Option τ → ℕ}
    {h : Option τ → ℤ} (hh : ∀ j, IsCoprime (h j) (q j : ℤ)) {M : ℝ} {l : ℕ} (hl : 0 < l)
    {U : MvPolynomial τ ℤ} (hU : U ∈ RSet M (fun j ↦ l * r (some j))) {V : Polynomial ℤ}
    (hV : V ≠ 0) (hVd : V.natDegree ≤ l * r none) (hVc : ∀ b, |(V.coeff b : ℝ)| ≤ M) :
    index (map (Int.castRingHom ℝ)
        (rename some U * Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℤ) V))
      (fun j ↦ (h j : ℝ) / q j) r ≤ l * lem6Φ q r M l := by
  classical
  set V' : MvPolynomial Unit ℤ := (uniqueAlgEquiv ℤ Unit).symm V with hV'
  have hV'0 : V' ≠ 0 := by
    rwa [hV', ne_eq, map_eq_zero_iff _ (uniqueAlgEquiv ℤ Unit).symm.injective]
  have hVmem : V' ∈ RSet M (fun _ : Unit ↦ l * r none) := by
    refine ⟨hV'0, fun j ↦ ?_, fun d ↦ ?_⟩
    · rw [Subsingleton.elim j default, hV', degreeOf_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm]
      exact hVd
    · rw [hV', MvPolynomial.coeff_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm]
      exact hVc _
  have hinjV : Function.Injective (fun _ : Unit ↦ (none : Option τ)) :=
    fun a b _ ↦ Subsingleton.elim a b
  have hmU : map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) U ≠ 0 := fun h0 ↦
    hU.1 (map_injective _ Int.cast_injective (h0.trans (map_zero _).symm))
  have hmV : map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) V' ≠ 0 := fun h0 ↦
    hV'0 (map_injective _ Int.cast_injective (h0.trans (map_zero _).symm))
  rw [aeval_X_eq_rename, _root_.map_mul, map_rename, map_rename,
    index_mul ((map_ne_zero_iff (rename (R := ℝ) some)
      (rename_injective _ (Option.some_injective _))).2 hmU)
      ((map_ne_zero_iff (rename (R := ℝ) (fun _ : Unit ↦ (none : Option τ)))
        (rename_injective _ hinjV)).2 hmV) _ hr0,
    index_rename (Option.some_injective _), index_rename hinjV]
  have hUΘ := index_le_Θ hU (h := h ∘ some)
    fun j ↦ hh (some j)
  have hVΘ := index_le_Θ hVmem
    (q := fun _ : Unit ↦ q none) (h := fun _ ↦ h none) fun _ ↦ hh none
  rw [IndexScale148.replacement] at hUΘ hVΘ
  have hlR : (0 : ℝ) < l := by exact_mod_cast hl
  rw [lem6Φ, mul_add, add_comm]
  exact add_le_add ((div_le_iff₀' hlR).1 hVΘ) ((div_le_iff₀' hlR).1 hUΘ)

#print axioms callerReplay
end IndexScaleCaller148

namespace IndexScaleBridge148
open MvPolynomial Roth
variable {σ : Type*} [Fintype σ]
theorem replacement {l : ℕ} (P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ) (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) :
    index P α (fun j ↦ l * r j) = index P α r / l := by
  rw [IndexConsumers133.targetBridge, IndexConsumers133.targetBridge, div_eq_inv_mul]
  have hw : (fun d : σ →₀ ℕ ↦ (weight (fun j ↦ l * r j) d : WithTop ℝ)) =
      fun d ↦ (((l : ℝ)⁻¹ * weight r d : ℝ) : WithTop ℝ) := by
    funext d
    rw [weight_mul_left, div_eq_inv_mul]
  rw [hw]
  exact IndexScaleMathlib148.scale (l : ℝ)⁻¹ (by positivity) (weight r) (taylor α P)
#print axioms replacement
end IndexScaleBridge148

namespace IndexScaleGuards148
universe u v w
theorem scaleGeneral {A : Type u} {R : Type v} {K : Type w}
    [Semiring R] [Semiring K] [LinearOrder K] [IsOrderedRing K]
    (c : K) (hc : 0 ≤ c) (D : A → K) (f : AddMonoidAlgebra R A) :
    (AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree (fun a ↦ (c * D a : WithTop K)) f).untopD 0 =
      c * (AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree (fun a ↦ (D a : WithTop K)) f).untopD 0 :=
  IndexScaleMathlib148.projectedTransport (c * ·)
    (fun _ _ h ↦ mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h hc) (mul_zero c) D f
theorem zeroFixingNecessary :
    (WithTop.map (fun x : ℝ ↦ x + 1) (⊤ : WithTop ℝ)).untopD 0 ≠
      (⊤ : WithTop ℝ).untopD 0 + 1 := by
  norm_num
theorem negativeScalingGuard :
    min ((-1 : ℝ) * 1) ((-1 : ℝ) * 2) ≠ (-1 : ℝ) * min 1 2 := by
  norm_num
#print axioms scaleGeneral
#print axioms zeroFixingNecessary
#print axioms negativeScalingGuard
end IndexScaleGuards148

    `Roth.exists_approximants,

    `Roth.abs_eval_lt_of_le_index,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.exists_roth_parameters_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.exists_roth_approximants_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.one_lt_roth_eval_bound_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.roth_product_bound_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.roth_log_bound_aux,
    `Roth.roth_of_isIntegral,
    `Roth.roth,

    `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.IsDiophantine,

      `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.exists_pos_div_rpow_lt_aux,
    `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.isDiophantine_iff,
    `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.thueSiegelRoth]
  let unique := names.toList.eraseDups.length
  unless names.size == 293 && unique == 293 do
    throwError "INDEX_RENAME149_BAD_CANONICAL_ARRAY"
  let mut present : Nat := 0
  let mut values : Nat := 0
  for n in names do
    match env.find? n with
    | none => throwError "INDEX_RENAME149_NOT_LOADED: {n}"
    | some ci =>
      present := present + 1
      match ci.value? (allowOpaque := true) with
      | none => pure ()
      | some val =>
        values := values + 1
        if val.getUsedConstants.any (fun c ↦ c == target) then
          Lean.logInfo m!"INDEX_RENAME149_DIRECT_CONSUMER: {n}"
  Lean.logInfo m!"INDEX_RENAME149_AUDIT: requested={names.size}; unique={unique}"
  Lean.logInfo m!"INDEX_RENAME149_AUDIT_PRESENT: present={present}; values={values}"
end IndexRenameAudit149

namespace IndexRenameMathlib149
open MvPolynomial
universe u v w z
#check @Finset.inf_image
#check @MvPolynomial.support_rename_of_injective
#check @Finsupp.linearCombination_mapDomain
#check @MvPolynomial.rename_eq_aeval
#check @MvPolynomial.comp_aeval_apply
-- Exact generic finite-support law; all variable and coefficient types arbitrary.
theorem transport {σ : Type u} {τ : Type v} {R : Type w} {T : Type z}
    [CommSemiring R] [SemilatticeInf T] [OrderTop T] {f : σ → τ}
    (hf : Function.Injective f) (D : (τ →₀ ℕ) → T) (P : MvPolynomial σ R) :
    AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree D (rename f P) =
      AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree (D ∘ Finsupp.mapDomain f) P := by
  classical
  exact (congrArg (fun s : Finset (τ →₀ ℕ) ↦ s.inf D)
    (support_rename_of_injective (p := P) hf)).trans
      (Finset.inf_image P.support (Finsupp.mapDomain f) D)
-- No injectivity is needed for transporting the weight alone.
theorem weightTransport {σ : Type u} {τ : Type v} {M : Type w}
    [AddCommMonoid M] (f : σ → τ) (w : τ → M) (d : σ →₀ ℕ) :
    Finsupp.weight w (d.mapDomain f) = Finsupp.weight (w ∘ f) d :=
  Finsupp.linearCombination_mapDomain ℕ (v' := w) f d
-- The center substitution is natural for every map; no project taylor theorem used.
theorem centerTransport {σ : Type u} {τ : Type v} {R : Type w}
    [CommSemiring R] (f : σ → τ) (α : τ → R) (P : MvPolynomial σ R) :
    aeval (fun j ↦ X j + C (α j)) (rename f P) =
      rename f (aeval (fun j ↦ X j + C ((α ∘ f) j)) P) := by
  simp only [rename_eq_aeval, comp_aeval_apply, Function.comp_apply,
    map_add, aeval_X, algHom_C, algebraMap_eq]
#print axioms transport
#print axioms weightTransport
#print axioms centerTransport
end IndexRenameMathlib149

namespace IndexRenameBridge149
open MvPolynomial
universe u v
-- Old interface proof intentionally avoids the target and parent taylor_rename/weight_mapDomain.
theorem replacement {σ : Type u} [Fintype σ] {τ : Type v} [Fintype τ]
    {f : σ → τ} (hf : Function.Injective f) (P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ)
    (α : τ → ℝ) (r : τ → ℕ) :
    Roth.index (rename f P) α r = Roth.index P (α ∘ f) (r ∘ f) := by
  rw [IndexExpanded133.fullyExpandedBridge, IndexExpanded133.fullyExpandedBridge]
  rw [IndexRenameMathlib149.centerTransport, IndexRenameMathlib149.transport hf]
  congr 2
  funext d
  exact congrArg WithTop.some
    (IndexRenameMathlib149.weightTransport f (fun j ↦ (r j : ℝ)⁻¹) d)
#print axioms replacement
end IndexRenameBridge149

namespace Roth
namespace IndexRenameCallers149
open MvPolynomial
variable {σ : Type*} [Fintype σ]
theorem equivCallerReplay {τ : Type*} [Fintype τ] (e : σ ≃ τ) (B : ℝ) (q r : τ → ℕ) :
    indexSet B (q ∘ e) (r ∘ e) ⊆ indexSet B q r := by
  rintro _ ⟨R, ⟨hR0, hRd, hRc⟩, h, hh, rfl⟩
  refine ⟨rename e R, ⟨?_, ?_, ?_⟩, h ∘ e.symm, ?_, ?_⟩
  · simpa [rename_eq_zero_iff_of_injective R e.injective] using hR0
  · intro j
    obtain ⟨i, rfl⟩ := e.surjective j
    simpa [degreeOf_rename_of_injective e.injective] using hRd i
  · intro d
    obtain ⟨d, rfl⟩ := Finsupp.mapDomain_surjective e.surjective d
    simpa [coeff_rename_mapDomain _ e.injective] using hRc d
  · intro j
    simpa using hh (e.symm j)
  · simp [map_rename, IndexRenameBridge149.replacement e.injective, Function.comp_def]

section
variable {τ : Type*} [Fintype τ]
theorem separatedCallerReplay {r : Option τ → ℕ} (hr0 : ∀ j, 0 < r j) {q : Option τ → ℕ}
    {h : Option τ → ℤ} (hh : ∀ j, IsCoprime (h j) (q j : ℤ)) {M : ℝ} {l : ℕ} (hl : 0 < l)
    {U : MvPolynomial τ ℤ} (hU : U ∈ RSet M (fun j ↦ l * r (some j))) {V : Polynomial ℤ}
    (hV : V ≠ 0) (hVd : V.natDegree ≤ l * r none) (hVc : ∀ b, |(V.coeff b : ℝ)| ≤ M) :
    index (map (Int.castRingHom ℝ)
        (rename some U * Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℤ) V))
      (fun j ↦ (h j : ℝ) / q j) r ≤ l * lem6Φ q r M l := by
  classical
  set V' : MvPolynomial Unit ℤ := (uniqueAlgEquiv ℤ Unit).symm V with hV'
  have hV'0 : V' ≠ 0 := by
    rwa [hV', ne_eq, map_eq_zero_iff _ (uniqueAlgEquiv ℤ Unit).symm.injective]
  have hVmem : V' ∈ RSet M (fun _ : Unit ↦ l * r none) := by
    refine ⟨hV'0, fun j ↦ ?_, fun d ↦ ?_⟩
    · rw [Subsingleton.elim j default, hV', degreeOf_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm]
      exact hVd
    · rw [hV', MvPolynomial.coeff_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm]
      exact hVc _
  have hinjV : Function.Injective (fun _ : Unit ↦ (none : Option τ)) :=
    fun a b _ ↦ Subsingleton.elim a b
  have hmU : map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) U ≠ 0 := fun h0 ↦
    hU.1 (map_injective _ Int.cast_injective (h0.trans (map_zero _).symm))
  have hmV : map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) V' ≠ 0 := fun h0 ↦
    hV'0 (map_injective _ Int.cast_injective (h0.trans (map_zero _).symm))
  rw [aeval_X_eq_rename, _root_.map_mul, map_rename, map_rename,
    index_mul ((map_ne_zero_iff (rename (R := ℝ) some)
      (rename_injective _ (Option.some_injective _))).2 hmU)
      ((map_ne_zero_iff (rename (R := ℝ) (fun _ : Unit ↦ (none : Option τ)))
        (rename_injective _ hinjV)).2 hmV) _ hr0,
    IndexRenameBridge149.replacement (Option.some_injective _),
    IndexRenameBridge149.replacement hinjV]
  have hUΘ := index_le_Θ hU (h := h ∘ some)
    fun j ↦ hh (some j)
  have hVΘ := index_le_Θ hVmem
    (q := fun _ : Unit ↦ q none) (h := fun _ ↦ h none) fun _ ↦ hh none
  rw [index_mul_left_weights] at hUΘ hVΘ
  have hlR : (0 : ℝ) < l := by exact_mod_cast hl
  rw [lem6Φ, mul_add, add_comm]
  exact add_le_add ((div_le_iff₀' hlR).1 hVΘ) ((div_le_iff₀' hlR).1 hUΘ)

end
#print axioms equivCallerReplay
#print axioms separatedCallerReplay
end IndexRenameCallers149
end Roth

namespace IndexRenameGuards149
open MvPolynomial
-- A noninjective map can cancel a nonzero polynomial; even positive weights do not help.
theorem collision :
    Roth.index (rename (fun _ : Fin 2 ↦ ()) (X 0 - X 1 : MvPolynomial (Fin 2) ℝ))
        (fun _ ↦ 0) (fun _ ↦ 1) ≠
      Roth.index (X 0 - X 1 : MvPolynomial (Fin 2) ℝ) (fun _ ↦ 0) (fun _ ↦ 1) := by
  have hP : (X 0 - X 1 : MvPolynomial (Fin 2) ℝ) ≠ 0 := by
    intro h
    have hcoeff := congrArg (fun P : MvPolynomial (Fin 2) ℝ ↦ P.coeff (Finsupp.single 0 1)) h
    simp at hcoeff
  have heval : eval (fun _ : Fin 2 ↦ (0 : ℝ)) (X 0 - X 1) = 0 := by simp
  have hn := (Roth.index_eq_zero_iff hP (r := fun _ ↦ 1) (fun _ ↦ by norm_num)
    (fun _ ↦ (0 : ℝ))).not.mpr (fun h ↦ h heval)
  simpa [Roth.index] using (Ne.symm hn)
-- Empty support and zero denominators require no extra assumptions in the actual old interface.
theorem zeroPolynomial {σ τ : Type*} [Fintype σ] [Fintype τ] {f : σ → τ}
    (hf : Function.Injective f) (α : τ → ℝ) (r : τ → ℕ) :
    Roth.index (rename f (0 : MvPolynomial σ ℝ)) α r =
      Roth.index (0 : MvPolynomial σ ℝ) (α ∘ f) (r ∘ f) :=
  IndexRenameBridge149.replacement hf 0 α r
theorem zeroWeights {σ τ : Type*} [Fintype σ] [Fintype τ] {f : σ → τ}
    (hf : Function.Injective f) (P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ) (α : τ → ℝ) :
    Roth.index (rename f P) α (fun _ ↦ 0) = Roth.index P (α ∘ f) (fun _ ↦ 0) :=
  IndexRenameBridge149.replacement hf P α (fun _ ↦ 0)
#print axioms collision
#print axioms zeroPolynomial
#print axioms zeroWeights
end IndexRenameGuards149

namespace IndexRenameExpanded149
open MvPolynomial
universe u v w z
theorem weighted {σ : Type u} {τ : Type v} {R : Type w} {M : Type z}
    [CommSemiring R] [AddCommMonoid M] [SemilatticeInf M] {f : σ → τ}
    (hf : Function.Injective f) (P : MvPolynomial σ R) (α : τ → R) (w : τ → M) :
    (AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree
      (fun d ↦ (Finsupp.weight w d : WithTop M))
      (aeval (fun j ↦ X j + C (α j)) (rename f P))).untopD 0 =
    (AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree
      (fun d ↦ (Finsupp.weight (w ∘ f) d : WithTop M))
      (aeval (fun j ↦ X j + C ((α ∘ f) j)) P)).untopD 0 := by
  rw [IndexRenameMathlib149.centerTransport, IndexRenameMathlib149.transport hf]
  congr 2
  funext d
  exact congrArg WithTop.some (IndexRenameMathlib149.weightTransport f w d)
#print axioms weighted
end IndexRenameExpanded149

#print axioms Roth.index_le_card
namespace IndexCardAudit150
run_cmd do
  let env ← Lean.getEnv
  let target := `Roth.index_le_card
  let names : Array Lean.Name := #[
    `MvPolynomial.hasseCoeff,
    `MvPolynomial.hasseDeriv,
    `MvPolynomial.hasseDeriv_monomial,

    `Roth.main_lemma,

    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.approxSet_num_den_mem_aux,
    `Roth.exists_reduced_approx,

    `Roth.exists_approximants,
    `Roth.exists_degrees,
    `Roth.one_le_prod_pow_mul_abs_eval,

    `Roth.abs_eval_lt_of_le_index,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.exists_roth_parameters_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.exists_roth_approximants_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.one_lt_roth_eval_bound_aux,

    `Roth.roth_of_isIntegral,
    `Roth.roth,

    `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.IsDiophantine,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.LeanEval
      `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.exists_pos_div_rpow_lt_aux,
    `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.isDiophantine_iff,
    `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.thueSiegelRoth]
  let unique := names.toList.eraseDups.length
  unless names.size == 293 && unique == 293 do
    throwError "INDEX_CARD150_BAD_CANONICAL_ARRAY"
  let mut present : Nat := 0
  let mut values : Nat := 0
  for n in names do
    match env.find? n with
    | none => throwError "INDEX_CARD150_NOT_LOADED: {n}"
    | some ci =>
      present := present + 1
      match ci.value? (allowOpaque := true) with
      | none => pure ()
      | some val =>
        values := values + 1
        if val.getUsedConstants.any (fun c ↦ c == target) then
          Lean.logInfo m!"INDEX_CARD150_DIRECT_CONSUMER: {n}"
  Lean.logInfo m!"INDEX_CARD150_AUDIT: requested={names.size}; unique={unique}"
  Lean.logInfo m!"INDEX_CARD150_AUDIT_PRESENT: present={present}; values={values}"
end IndexCardAudit150

namespace IndexCardBridge150
open MvPolynomial Roth
variable {σ : Type*} [Fintype σ]
theorem noNonzero {P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ} {r : σ → ℕ}
    (hd : ∀ j, degreeOf j P ≤ r j) (α : σ → ℝ) :
    index P α r ≤ Fintype.card σ := by
  classical
  by_cases hP : P = 0
  · subst P
    simp [index]
  · obtain ⟨d, hd0, hdw⟩ := exists_weight_eq_index hP α r
    have hdmem : d ∈ (taylor α P).support := by
      rw [mem_support_iff, coeff_taylor]
      exact hd0
    exact hdw ▸ weight_le_card fun j ↦
      (monomial_le_degreeOf j hdmem).trans ((degreeOf_taylor_le α j P).trans (hd j))

theorem oneWitness {P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ} {r : σ → ℕ} {α : σ → ℝ}
    (h : ∃ d, eval α (hasseDeriv d P) ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ j, d j ≤ r j) :
    index P α r ≤ Fintype.card σ := by
  obtain ⟨d, hd, hdr⟩ := h
  exact (index_le_weight hd).trans (weight_le_card hdr)

theorem weightedWitness {P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ} {r : σ → ℕ} {α : σ → ℝ} {c : ℝ}
    (h : ∃ d, eval α (hasseDeriv d P) ≠ 0 ∧ weight r d ≤ c) : index P α r ≤ c := by
  obtain ⟨d, hd, hdc⟩ := h
  exact (index_le_weight hd).trans hdc
#print axioms noNonzero
#print axioms oneWitness
#print axioms weightedWitness
end IndexCardBridge150

namespace IndexCardCallers150
open MvPolynomial Roth
variable {σ : Type*} [Fintype σ]
theorem bddAboveReplay (B : ℝ) (q : σ → ℕ) {r : σ → ℕ} : BddAbove (indexSet B q r) := by
  classical
  refine ⟨Fintype.card σ, ?_⟩
  rintro _ ⟨R, ⟨-, hRd, -⟩, h, -, rfl⟩
  exact IndexCardBridge150.noNonzero
    (by simpa [degreeOf, degrees_map_of_injective R (f := Int.castRingHom ℝ)
          Int.cast_injective] using hRd) _

theorem cardReplay {B : ℝ} (q : σ → ℕ) {r : σ → ℕ} : Θ B q r ≤ Fintype.card σ := by
  refine Real.sSup_le ?_ (Nat.cast_nonneg _)
  rintro _ ⟨R, ⟨-, hRd, -⟩, h, -, rfl⟩
  exact IndexCardBridge150.noNonzero
    (by simpa [degreeOf, degrees_map_of_injective R (f := Int.castRingHom ℝ)
          Int.cast_injective] using hRd) _

#print axioms bddAboveReplay
#print axioms cardReplay
end IndexCardCallers150

namespace IndexCardGuards150
open Roth MvPolynomial
theorem zeroPolynomial {σ : Type*} [Fintype σ] (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) :
    index (0 : MvPolynomial σ ℝ) α r = 0 := by simp [index]
theorem zeroWeights {σ : Type*} [Fintype σ] (P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ) (α : σ → ℝ) :
    index P α (fun _ ↦ 0) = 0 := by
  simpa only [Nat.zero_mul] using IndexScale148.zeroScale P α (fun _ ↦ 1)
theorem squareIndex : index (monomial (Finsupp.single () 2) 1 : MvPolynomial Unit ℝ)
    (fun _ ↦ 0) (fun _ ↦ 1) = 2 := by
  simp [index, weight, taylor_zero, support_monomial]
theorem degreeBoundNecessary : ¬ index (monomial (Finsupp.single () 2) 1 : MvPolynomial Unit ℝ)
    (fun _ ↦ 0) (fun _ ↦ 1) ≤ Fintype.card Unit := by
  simp [index, weight, taylor_zero, support_monomial]
theorem infDegreeZeroGuard : ¬ (0 : AddMonoidAlgebra ℝ Unit).infDegree
    (fun _ ↦ (0 : WithTop ℝ)) ≤ (1 : WithTop ℝ) := by
  simp [AddMonoidAlgebra.infDegree]
theorem negativeEmptyGuard : ¬ sInf ((fun _ : Unit ↦ (0 : ℝ)) '' (∅ : Set Unit)) ≤ -1 := by simp
#print axioms zeroPolynomial
#print axioms zeroWeights
#print axioms squareIndex
#print axioms degreeBoundNecessary
#print axioms infDegreeZeroGuard
#print axioms negativeEmptyGuard
end IndexCardGuards150

#print Roth.index_unique
#print axioms Roth.index_unique
