    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.MainLemma `Roth.exists_auxiliary_polynomial_of_log_bound_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.MainLemma `Roth.theta_lt_eta_of_sq_log_bound_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.MainLemma `Roth.exists_auxiliary_polynomial_with_index_gap_aux,
    `Roth.main_lemma,
    `Roth.approxSet,
    `Roth.exists_nat_mul_isIntegral,
    `Roth.approxSet_den_le_finite,
    `Roth.eventually_div_rpow_lt,
    `Roth.reduction,
    `Roth.exists_monic_aeval_eq_zero,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.approxSet_num_den_mem_aux,
    `Roth.exists_reduced_approx,
    `Roth.exists_m,
    `Roth.exists_delta,
    `Roth.exists_approximants,
    `Roth.exists_degrees,
    `Roth.one_le_prod_pow_mul_abs_eval,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.abs_prod_pow_le_rpow_of_le_weight,
    `Roth.abs_eval_lt_of_le_index,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.exists_roth_parameters_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.exists_roth_approximants_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.one_lt_roth_eval_bound_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.roth_product_bound_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.roth_log_bound_aux,
    `Roth.roth_of_isIntegral,
    `Roth.roth,
    `Roth.roth_rat,
    `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.IsDiophantine,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.LeanEval
      `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.exists_pos_div_rpow_lt_aux,
    `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.isDiophantine_iff,
    `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.thueSiegelRoth]
  let unique := names.toList.eraseDups.length
  unless names.size == 293 && unique == 293 do
    throwError "COEFFICIENT_EXPANSION156_BAD_CANONICAL_ARRAY"
  let mut present : Nat := 0
  let mut values : Nat := 0
  for n in names do
    match env.find? n with
    | none => throwError "COEFFICIENT_EXPANSION156_NOT_LOADED: {n}"
    | some ci =>
      present := present + 1
      match ci.value? (allowOpaque := true) with
      | none => pure ()
      | some val =>
        values := values + 1
        if val.getUsedConstants.any (fun c ↦ c == target) then
          Lean.logInfo m!"COEFFICIENT_EXPANSION156_DIRECT_CONSUMER: {n}"
  Lean.logInfo m!"COEFFICIENT_EXPANSION156_AUDIT: requested={names.size}; unique={unique}"
  Lean.logInfo m!"COEFFICIENT_EXPANSION156_AUDIT_PRESENT: present={present}; values={values}"
end CoefficientExpansionAudit156

namespace Expansion156
open MvPolynomial
variable {τ R : Type*} [CommSemiring R]
theorem canonical (P : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) {n : ℕ}
    (hn : degreeOf none P ≤ n) :
    optionEquivLeft R τ P = ∑ ν ∈ Finset.range (n + 1),
      Polynomial.C ((optionEquivLeft R τ P).coeff ν) * Polynomial.X ^ ν :=
  Polynomial.as_sum_range_C_mul_X_pow' _
    (Nat.lt_succ_of_le ((natDegree_optionEquivLeft R P).trans_le hn))
theorem inverse_C (Q : MvPolynomial τ R) :
    (optionEquivLeft R τ).symm (Polynomial.C Q) = rename some Q := by
  change Polynomial.aevalTower (rename some) (X none) (Polynomial.C Q) = _
  simp
theorem expanded (P : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) {n : ℕ}
    (hn : degreeOf none P ≤ n) :
    P = ∑ ν ∈ Finset.range (n + 1),
      X none ^ ν * rename some ((optionEquivLeft R τ P).coeff ν) := by
  have h := congrArg (optionEquivLeft R τ).symm (canonical P hn)
  simpa only [AlgEquiv.symm_apply_apply, map_sum, map_mul, map_pow,
    optionEquivLeft_symm_X, inverse_C, mul_comm] using h
#print axioms canonical
#print axioms inverse_C
#print axioms expanded
end Expansion156

namespace Expansion156Caller
open MvPolynomial
variable {τ K : Type*} [Field K]
open Lean Elab Term in
elab "originalBasis156" : term => do
  return ← Lean.Meta.mkConstWithFreshMVarLevels
    (Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Separation `Roth.exists_coeffNone_basis_aux)
open Lean Elab Term in
elab "originalIndependent156" : term => do
  return ← Lean.Meta.mkConstWithFreshMVarLevels
    (Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Separation `Roth.linearIndependent_coordinate_polynomials_aux)
open Lean Elab Term in
elab "originalSumBasis156" : term => do
  return ← Lean.Meta.mkConstWithFreshMVarLevels
    (Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Separation `Roth.eq_sum_aeval_basis_coeffNone_aux)
theorem exists_separated_representation {P : MvPolynomial (Option τ) K} (hP : P ≠ 0) {n : ℕ}
    (hn : degreeOf none P ≤ n) :
    ∃ l, 1 ≤ l ∧ l ≤ n + 1 ∧ ∃ (φ : Fin l → Polynomial K) (ψ : Fin l → MvPolynomial τ K),
      LinearIndependent K φ ∧ LinearIndependent K ψ ∧ (∀ ν, (φ ν).natDegree ≤ n) ∧
      (∀ ν j, degreeOf j (ψ ν) ≤ degreeOf (some j) P) ∧
      P = ∑ ν, Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) K) (φ ν) * rename some (ψ ν) := by
  classical
  let v : Fin (n + 1) → MvPolynomial τ K := fun ν ↦ Roth.coeffNone P ν
  have hP' : P = ∑ ν : Fin (n + 1), X none ^ (ν : ℕ) * rename some (v ν) := by
    rw [Fin.sum_univ_eq_sum_range (fun ν ↦ X none ^ ν * rename some (Roth.coeffNone P ν))]
    exact Expansion156.expanded P hn
  obtain ⟨l, b, hl1, hl2, hdegree⟩ := originalBasis156 hP hP'
  refine ⟨l, hl1, hl2, fun ρ ↦ ∑ ν : Fin (n + 1),
      Polynomial.C (b.repr ⟨v ν, Submodule.subset_span ⟨ν, rfl⟩⟩ ρ) * Polynomial.X ^ (ν : ℕ),
    fun ρ ↦ (b ρ : MvPolynomial τ K), originalIndependent156 v b,
    b.linearIndependent.map' (Submodule.span K (Set.range v)).subtype
      (Submodule.ker_subtype _), ?_, hdegree,
    originalSumBasis156 hP' b⟩
  exact fun ρ ↦ Polynomial.natDegree_sum_le_of_forall_le _ _ fun ν _ ↦
    (Polynomial.natDegree_C_mul_X_pow_le _ _).trans (Nat.lt_succ_iff.1 ν.2)

#print axioms exists_separated_representation
end Expansion156Caller

    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.MainLemma `Roth.exists_auxiliary_polynomial_of_log_bound_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.MainLemma `Roth.theta_lt_eta_of_sq_log_bound_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.MainLemma `Roth.exists_auxiliary_polynomial_with_index_gap_aux,
    `Roth.main_lemma,
    `Roth.approxSet,
    `Roth.exists_nat_mul_isIntegral,
    `Roth.approxSet_den_le_finite,
    `Roth.eventually_div_rpow_lt,
    `Roth.reduction,
    `Roth.exists_monic_aeval_eq_zero,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.approxSet_num_den_mem_aux,
    `Roth.exists_reduced_approx,
    `Roth.exists_m,
    `Roth.exists_delta,
    `Roth.exists_approximants,
    `Roth.exists_degrees,
    `Roth.one_le_prod_pow_mul_abs_eval,

    `Roth.abs_eval_lt_of_le_index,

    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.roth_product_bound_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.roth_log_bound_aux,
    `Roth.roth_of_isIntegral,
    `Roth.roth,
    `Roth.roth_rat,
    `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.IsDiophantine,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.LeanEval
      `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.exists_pos_div_rpow_lt_aux,
    `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.isDiophantine_iff,
    `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.thueSiegelRoth]
  let unique := names.toList.eraseDups.length
  unless names.size == 293 && unique == 293 do
    throwError "COEFFICIENT_DEGREE157_BAD_CANONICAL_ARRAY"
  let mut present : Nat := 0
  let mut values : Nat := 0
  for n in names do
    match env.find? n with
    | none => throwError "COEFFICIENT_DEGREE157_NOT_LOADED: {n}"
    | some ci =>
      present := present + 1
      match ci.value? (allowOpaque := true) with
      | none => pure ()
      | some val =>
        values := values + 1
        if val.getUsedConstants.any (fun c ↦ c == target) then
          Lean.logInfo m!"COEFFICIENT_DEGREE157_DIRECT_CONSUMER: {n}"
  Lean.logInfo m!"COEFFICIENT_DEGREE157_AUDIT: requested={names.size}; unique={unique}"
  Lean.logInfo m!"COEFFICIENT_DEGREE157_AUDIT_PRESENT: present={present}; values={values}"
end CoefficientDegreeAudit157

namespace CoefficientDegree157Caller
open MvPolynomial Roth
variable {τ : Type*} {K : Type*} [Field K]
theorem canonical (P : MvPolynomial (Option τ) K) (ν : ℕ) (j : τ) :
    degreeOf j (coeffNone P ν) ≤ degreeOf (some j) P := by
  refine degreeOf_le_iff.2 fun d hd ↦ ?_
  simpa using monomial_le_degreeOf (some j) ((mem_support_coeff_optionEquivLeft K).1 hd)
theorem exists_coeffNone_basis_aux {P : MvPolynomial (Option τ) K} (hP : P ≠ 0) {n : ℕ}
    (hP' : P = ∑ ν : Fin (n + 1), X none ^ (ν : ℕ) * rename some (coeffNone P ν)) :
    ∃ l, ∃ b : Module.Basis (Fin l) K
      (Submodule.span K (Set.range (fun ν : Fin (n + 1) ↦ coeffNone P ν))),
      1 ≤ l ∧ l ≤ n + 1 ∧ ∀ ρ j, degreeOf j (b ρ : MvPolynomial τ K) ≤ degreeOf (some j) P := by
  classical
  let v : Fin (n + 1) → MvPolynomial τ K := fun ν ↦ coeffNone P ν
  let V := Submodule.span K (Set.range v)
  have : FiniteDimensional K V := FiniteDimensional.span_of_finite K (Set.finite_range v)
  let b := Module.finBasis K V
  have hl1 : 1 ≤ Module.finrank K V := by
    obtain ⟨ν, hν⟩ : ∃ ν, v ν ≠ 0 := by
      by_contra! h
      apply hP
      change P = ∑ ν : Fin (n + 1), X none ^ (ν : ℕ) * rename some (v ν) at hP'
      rw [hP']
      simp [h]
    exact Module.finrank_pos_iff_exists_ne_zero.2
      ⟨⟨v ν, Submodule.subset_span ⟨ν, rfl⟩⟩, fun h ↦ hν (congrArg Subtype.val h)⟩
  have hdegree : V ≤ restrictSupport K {d | ∀ j, d j ≤ degreeOf (some j) P} :=
    Submodule.span_le.2 (Set.range_subset_iff.2 fun (ν : Fin (n + 1)) d hd j ↦ by
      exact degreeOf_le_iff.1 (canonical P ν j) d hd)
  exact ⟨Module.finrank K V, b, hl1,
    (finrank_range_le_card (R := K) v).trans_eq (Fintype.card_fin _),
    fun ρ j ↦ degreeOf_le_iff.2 fun d hd ↦ hdegree (b ρ).2 hd j⟩

#print axioms CoefficientDegree157Caller.canonical
#print axioms CoefficientDegree157Caller.exists_coeffNone_basis_aux
end CoefficientDegree157Caller

elab "originalIndexSeparated158" : term => do
  return ← Lean.Meta.mkConstWithFreshMVarLevels
    (Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Theta `Roth.index_separated_mul_le_aux)
theorem oldForm (U : MvPolynomial τ R) (V : Polynomial R) (a : τ →₀ ℕ) (b : ℕ) :
    (rename some U * Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) V).coeff
        (a.mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none b) = U.coeff a * V.coeff b := by
  have h : (optionEquivLeft R τ).toAlgHom.comp (rename some) = Polynomial.CAlgHom := by
    ext i
    simp
  have he : a.optionElim b = a.mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none b :=
    (Finsupp.update_eq_add_single (Finsupp.embDomain_some_none a) b).trans
      (congrArg (fun e ↦ e + Finsupp.single none b) (Finsupp.embDomain_eq_mapDomain .some a))
  rw [← he, ← optionEquivLeft_coeff_coeff, map_mul,
    (show optionEquivLeft R τ (rename some U) = Polynomial.C U from AlgHom.congr_fun h U),
    ← Polynomial.aeval_algHom_apply, optionEquivLeft_X_none, Polynomial.aeval_X_left_eq_map,
    Polynomial.coeff_C_mul, Polynomial.coeff_map, algebraMap_eq, mul_comm U, coeff_C_mul, mul_comm]

#print axioms oldForm
theorem exists_int_polynomial_of_primitive_factor_aux {U : MvPolynomial τ ℤ}
    {V' : Polynomial ℚ} {F : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℤ} (hgcd : U.support.gcd U.coeff = 1)
    (hF : map (Int.castRingHom ℚ) F = rename some (map (Int.castRingHom ℚ) U) *
      Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℚ) V') :
    ∃ V : Polynomial ℤ, V.map (Int.castRingHom ℚ) = V' := by
  apply (Polynomial.mem_map_range (Int.castRingHom ℚ)).2
  intro b
  obtain ⟨z, hz⟩ := exists_int_eq_of_gcd_eq_one hgcd fun a _ ↦
    ⟨F.coeff (a.mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none b), by
      have h := congrArg (fun P ↦ P.coeff (a.mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none b)) hF
      simp only [oldForm, coeff_map] at h
      simpa using h.symm⟩
  exact ⟨z, hz.symm⟩


#print axioms exists_int_polynomial_of_primitive_factor_aux
variable [Fintype τ]
theorem index_det_le {r : Option τ → ℕ} (hr0 : ∀ j, 0 < r j) {r₁ : ℕ} (hr₁ : ∀ j, r j ≤ r₁)
    {q : Option τ → ℕ} {B : ℝ} {R : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℤ} (hR : R ∈ RSet B r)
    {h : Option τ → ℤ} (hh : ∀ j, IsCoprime (h j) (q j : ℤ)) {l : ℕ} (hl : 1 ≤ l)
    {D : Fin l → (τ →₀ ℕ)} {U : MvPolynomial τ ℤ} {V : Polynomial ℤ} (hU : U ≠ 0) (hV : V ≠ 0)
    (hUd : ∀ j, degreeOf j U ≤ l * r (some j)) (hVd : V.natDegree ≤ l * r none)
    (hF : (Matrix.of fun μ ν : Fin l ↦
        hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) R).det =
      rename some U * Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℤ) V) :
    index (map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) (Matrix.of fun μ ν : Fin l ↦
        hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) R).det)
      (fun j ↦ (h j : ℝ) / q j) r ≤ l * lem6Φ q r (lem6M (Fintype.card τ + 1) r₁ B l) l := by
  classical
  -- Step 1: the coefficients of `F` are bounded by `M`.
  have hFcoef (d) : |(((Matrix.of fun μ ν : Fin l ↦ hasseDeriv
      ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) R).det.coeff d : ℤ) : ℝ)| ≤
      lem6M (Fintype.card τ + 1) r₁ B l := by
    simpa only [lem6M, Finset.prod_const, Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ,
      Fintype.card_option, nsmul_eq_mul, Nat.cast_id, pow_mul, mul_right_comm] using
      abs_coeff_det_hasseDeriv_le _ R (r := fun _ ↦ r₁) (fun j ↦ (hR.2.1 j).trans (hr₁ j))
        ((abs_nonneg ((R.coeff 0 : ℤ) : ℝ)).trans (hR.2.2 0)) hR.2.2 d
  -- Step 2: the coefficients of `U` and `V` are bounded by `M`.
  have hUc : ∀ a, |((U.coeff a : ℤ) : ℝ)| ≤ lem6M (Fintype.card τ + 1) r₁ B l := fun a ↦ by
    have h1 := hFcoef (a.mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none V.natDegree)
    rw [hF, oldForm, Int.cast_mul, abs_mul] at h1
    exact (le_mul_of_one_le_right (abs_nonneg _) (by
      exact_mod_cast Int.one_le_abs (Polynomial.leadingCoeff_ne_zero.2 hV))).trans h1
  have hVc : ∀ b, |((V.coeff b : ℤ) : ℝ)| ≤ lem6M (Fintype.card τ + 1) r₁ B l := fun b ↦ by
    obtain ⟨a₀, ha₀⟩ := MvPolynomial.ne_zero_iff.1 hU
    have h1 := hFcoef (a₀.mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none b)
    rw [hF, oldForm, Int.cast_mul, abs_mul] at h1
    exact (le_mul_of_one_le_left (abs_nonneg _) (by
      exact_mod_cast Int.one_le_abs ha₀)).trans h1
  -- Step 3: Lemma 4-2, the index of the product of polynomials in separate variables.
  rw [hF]
  exact originalIndexSeparated158 hr0 hh hl ⟨hU, hUd, hUc⟩ hV hVd hVc


#print axioms index_det_le
end SeparatedProductReplay158

    | none => throwError "AEVAL_RENAME159_NOT_LOADED: {n}"

        if val.getUsedConstants.any (fun c ↦ c == target) then
          Lean.logInfo m!"AEVAL_RENAME159_DIRECT_CONSUMER: {n}"
  Lean.logInfo m!"AEVAL_RENAME159_AUDIT: requested={names.size}; unique={unique}"
  Lean.logInfo m!"AEVAL_RENAME159_AUDIT_PRESENT: present={present}; values={values}"
end AevalRenameAudit159

namespace AevalReplay159
open Roth MvPolynomial
section Semiring
variable {R : Type*} [CommSemiring R]
theorem oldForm {σ : Type*} (i : σ) (f : Polynomial R) :
    Polynomial.aeval (X i : MvPolynomial σ R) f =
      rename (fun _ : Unit ↦ i) ((uniqueAlgEquiv R Unit).symm f) :=
  AlgHom.congr_fun (Polynomial.toMvPolynomial_eq_rename_comp i) f
variable {τ : Type*}
theorem hasseDeriv_rename_mul_aeval (d : τ →₀ ℕ) (ν : ℕ) (U : MvPolynomial τ R) (V : Polynomial R) :
    hasseDeriv (d.mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none ν) (rename some U * Polynomial.aeval
      (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) V) = rename some (hasseDeriv d U) * Polynomial.aeval
      (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) (Polynomial.hasseDeriv ν V) := by
  classical
  have hnone : ∀ {o : Option τ}, o ∈ (d.mapDomain some).support → o ≠ none := by
    simp
  have hdisj : Disjoint (d.mapDomain some).support (Finsupp.single none ν).support :=
    Finset.disjoint_left.2 fun _ ho ho' ↦
      hnone ho (Finset.mem_singleton.1 (Finsupp.support_single_subset ho'))
  rw [← hasseDeriv_hasseDeriv_of_disjoint hdisj,
    hasseDeriv_mul_of_degreeOf_eq_zero fun j hj ↦ by
      rw [Finset.mem_singleton.1 (Finsupp.support_single_subset hj)]
      simp [degreeOf, degrees_rename_of_injective (Option.some_injective τ)],
    hasseDeriv_single_aeval_X, mul_comm,
    hasseDeriv_mul_of_degreeOf_eq_zero fun j hj ↦ by
      simp [oldForm, degreeOf,
        degrees_rename_of_injective (Function.injective_of_subsingleton _), Multiset.mem_replicate,
        hnone hj],
    hasseDeriv_rename (Option.some_injective τ), mul_comm]

end Semiring
section Domain
variable {R τ : Type*} [CommRing R] [IsDomain R]
theorem degreeOf_rename_mul_aeval (U : MvPolynomial τ R) {V : Polynomial R} (hV : V ≠ 0) (j : τ) :
    degreeOf (some j) (rename some U * Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) V) =
      degreeOf j U := by
  classical
  by_cases hU : U = 0
  · simp [hU]
  rw [degreeOf_mul_eq ((rename_eq_zero_iff_of_injective U (Option.some_injective τ)).not.mpr hU)
      ((map_ne_zero_iff (Polynomial.aeval (X none))
        (Polynomial.toMvPolynomial_injective none)).2 hV),
    degreeOf_rename_of_injective (Option.some_injective τ)]
  simp [oldForm, degreeOf,
    degrees_rename_of_injective (Function.injective_of_subsingleton _), Multiset.mem_replicate]

theorem degreeOf_none_rename_mul_aeval {U : MvPolynomial τ R} (hU : U ≠ 0) (V : Polynomial R) :
    degreeOf none (rename some U * Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) V) =
      V.natDegree := by
  classical
  by_cases hV : V = 0
  · simp [hV]
  rw [degreeOf_mul_eq ((rename_eq_zero_iff_of_injective U (Option.some_injective τ)).not.mpr hU)
      ((map_ne_zero_iff (Polynomial.aeval (X none))
        (Polynomial.toMvPolynomial_injective none)).2 hV),
    (show degreeOf none (rename some U) = 0 by
      simp [degreeOf, degrees_rename_of_injective (Option.some_injective τ)]),
    zero_add, oldForm]
  exact (degreeOf_rename_of_injective (f := fun _ : Unit ↦ (none : Option τ))
    (fun _ _ _ ↦ rfl) ()).trans (degreeOf_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm V)

end Domain
section FiniteTheta
variable {τ : Type*} [Fintype τ]
theorem index_separated_mul_le_aux {r : Option τ → ℕ} (hr0 : ∀ j, 0 < r j) {q : Option τ → ℕ}
    {h : Option τ → ℤ} (hh : ∀ j, IsCoprime (h j) (q j : ℤ)) {M : ℝ} {l : ℕ} (hl : 0 < l)
    {U : MvPolynomial τ ℤ} (hU : U ∈ RSet M (fun j ↦ l * r (some j))) {V : Polynomial ℤ}
    (hV : V ≠ 0) (hVd : V.natDegree ≤ l * r none) (hVc : ∀ b, |(V.coeff b : ℝ)| ≤ M) :
    index (map (Int.castRingHom ℝ)
        (rename some U * Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℤ) V))
      (fun j ↦ (h j : ℝ) / q j) r ≤ l * lem6Φ q r M l := by
  classical
  set V' : MvPolynomial Unit ℤ := (uniqueAlgEquiv ℤ Unit).symm V with hV'
  have hV'0 : V' ≠ 0 := by
    rwa [hV', ne_eq, map_eq_zero_iff _ (uniqueAlgEquiv ℤ Unit).symm.injective]
  have hVmem : V' ∈ RSet M (fun _ : Unit ↦ l * r none) := by
    refine ⟨hV'0, fun j ↦ ?_, fun d ↦ ?_⟩
    · rw [Subsingleton.elim j default, hV', degreeOf_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm]
      exact hVd
    · rw [hV', MvPolynomial.coeff_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm]
      exact hVc _
  have hinjV : Function.Injective (fun _ : Unit ↦ (none : Option τ)) :=
    fun a b _ ↦ Subsingleton.elim a b
  have hmU : map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) U ≠ 0 := fun h0 ↦
    hU.1 (map_injective _ Int.cast_injective (h0.trans (map_zero _).symm))
  have hmV : map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) V' ≠ 0 := fun h0 ↦
    hV'0 (map_injective _ Int.cast_injective (h0.trans (map_zero _).symm))
  rw [oldForm, _root_.map_mul, map_rename, map_rename,
    index_mul ((map_ne_zero_iff (rename (R := ℝ) some)
      (rename_injective _ (Option.some_injective _))).2 hmU)
      ((map_ne_zero_iff (rename (R := ℝ) (fun _ : Unit ↦ (none : Option τ)))
        (rename_injective _ hinjV)).2 hmV) _ hr0,
    index_rename (Option.some_injective _), index_rename hinjV]
  have hUΘ := index_le_Θ hU (h := h ∘ some)
    fun j ↦ hh (some j)
  have hVΘ := index_le_Θ hVmem
    (q := fun _ : Unit ↦ q none) (h := fun _ ↦ h none) fun _ ↦ hh none
  rw [index_mul_left_weights] at hUΘ hVΘ
  have hlR : (0 : ℝ) < l := by exact_mod_cast hl
  rw [lem6Φ, mul_add, add_comm]
  exact add_le_add ((div_le_iff₀' hlR).1 hVΘ) ((div_le_iff₀' hlR).1 hUΘ)

end FiniteTheta
section Mapped
variable {τ : Type*}
theorem mapped_det_ne_zero_aux {R : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℤ} {l : ℕ} {D : Fin l → (τ →₀ ℕ)}
    {U : MvPolynomial τ ℤ} {V : Polynomial ℤ} (hU : U ≠ 0) (hV : V ≠ 0)
    (hF : (Matrix.of fun μ ν : Fin l ↦
      hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) R).det =
        rename some U * Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℤ) V) :
    (Matrix.of fun μ ν : Fin l ↦ hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some +
      Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) (map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) R)).det ≠ 0 := by
  have hmapdet : map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) (Matrix.of fun μ ν : Fin l ↦
      hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) R).det =
      (Matrix.of fun μ ν : Fin l ↦ hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some +
        Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) (map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) R)).det := by
    rw [RingHom.map_det]
    congr 1
    exact Matrix.ext fun μ ν ↦ by simp [hasseDeriv_map]
  rw [← hmapdet, ne_eq, map_eq_zero_iff _ (map_injective _ Int.cast_injective), hF,
    mul_eq_zero, not_or, map_eq_zero_iff _ (rename_injective _ (Option.some_injective _))]
  refine ⟨hU, ?_⟩
  rw [oldForm, map_eq_zero_iff _ (rename_injective _ fun a b _ ↦ Subsingleton.elim a b)]
  rwa [map_eq_zero_iff _ (uniqueAlgEquiv ℤ Unit).symm.injective]

end Mapped

#print axioms mapped_det_ne_zero_aux

    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.MainLemma `Roth.abs_coeff_diag_hasseDeriv_modByMonic_le_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.MainLemma `Roth.abs_coeff_diag_hasseDeriv_modByMonic_le_floor_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.MainLemma `Roth.exists_mem_RSet_le_index_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.MainLemma `Roth.mul_card_filter_weight_le_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.MainLemma `Roth.exists_mem_RSet_le_index_of_rpow_bound_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.MainLemma `Roth.exists_mem_RSet_le_index_of_log_bound_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.MainLemma `Roth.exists_auxiliary_polynomial_of_log_bound_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.MainLemma `Roth.theta_lt_eta_of_sq_log_bound_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.MainLemma `Roth.exists_auxiliary_polynomial_with_index_gap_aux,
    `Roth.main_lemma,
    `Roth.approxSet,
    `Roth.exists_nat_mul_isIntegral,
    `Roth.approxSet_den_le_finite,
    `Roth.eventually_div_rpow_lt,
    `Roth.reduction,
    `Roth.exists_monic_aeval_eq_zero,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.approxSet_num_den_mem_aux,
    `Roth.exists_reduced_approx,
    `Roth.exists_m,
    `Roth.exists_delta,
    `Roth.exists_approximants,
    `Roth.exists_degrees,
    `Roth.one_le_prod_pow_mul_abs_eval,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.abs_prod_pow_le_rpow_of_le_weight,
    `Roth.abs_eval_lt_of_le_index,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.exists_roth_parameters_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.exists_roth_approximants_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.one_lt_roth_eval_bound_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.roth_product_bound_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.roth_log_bound_aux,
    `Roth.roth_of_isIntegral,
    `Roth.roth,
    `Roth.roth_rat,
    `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.IsDiophantine,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.LeanEval
      `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.exists_pos_div_rpow_lt_aux,
    `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.isDiophantine_iff,
    `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.thueSiegelRoth]
  let unique := names.toList.eraseDups.length
  unless names.size == 293 && unique == 293 do
    throwError "HASSE_SEPARATION160_BAD_CANONICAL_ARRAY"
  let mut present : Nat := 0
  let mut values : Nat := 0
  for n in names do
    match env.find? n with
    | none => throwError "HASSE_SEPARATION160_NOT_LOADED: {n}"
    | some ci =>
      present := present + 1
      match ci.value? (allowOpaque := true) with
      | none => pure ()
      | some val =>
        values := values + 1
        if val.getUsedConstants.any (fun c ↦ c == target) then
          Lean.logInfo m!"HASSE_SEPARATION160_DIRECT_CONSUMER: {n}"
  Lean.logInfo m!"HASSE_SEPARATION160_AUDIT: requested={names.size}; unique={unique}"
  Lean.logInfo m!"HASSE_SEPARATION160_AUDIT_PRESENT: present={present}; values={values}"
end HasseSeparationAudit160

namespace HasseSeparation160
open MvPolynomial
variable {τ R : Type*} [CommSemiring R]
theorem factorwise (d : τ →₀ ℕ) (ν : ℕ) (U : MvPolynomial τ R) (V : Polynomial R) :
    hasseDeriv (d.mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none ν) (rename some U * Polynomial.aeval
      (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) V) = rename some (hasseDeriv d U) * Polynomial.aeval
      (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) (Polynomial.hasseDeriv ν V) := by
  classical
  fail_if_success
    exact Polynomial.hasseDeriv_mul _ _ _
  fail_if_success
    solve | simp only [Polynomial.toMvPolynomial_eq_rename_comp]
  have hnone : ∀ {o : Option τ}, o ∈ (d.mapDomain some).support → o ≠ none := by
    simp
  have hdisj : Disjoint (d.mapDomain some).support (Finsupp.single none ν).support :=
    Finset.disjoint_left.2 fun _ ho ho' ↦
      hnone ho (Finset.mem_singleton.1 (Finsupp.support_single_subset ho'))
  rw [← hasseDeriv_hasseDeriv_of_disjoint hdisj,
    hasseDeriv_mul_of_degreeOf_eq_zero fun j hj ↦ by
      rw [Finset.mem_singleton.1 (Finsupp.support_single_subset hj)]
      simp [degreeOf, degrees_rename_of_injective (Option.some_injective τ)],
    hasseDeriv_single_aeval_X, mul_comm,
    hasseDeriv_mul_of_degreeOf_eq_zero fun j hj ↦ by
      simp [show ∀ f : Polynomial R, Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) f =
          rename (fun _ : Unit ↦ none) ((uniqueAlgEquiv R Unit).symm f) from
          fun f ↦ AlgHom.congr_fun (Polynomial.toMvPolynomial_eq_rename_comp none) f, degreeOf,
        degrees_rename_of_injective (Function.injective_of_subsingleton _), Multiset.mem_replicate,
        hnone hj],
    hasseDeriv_rename (Option.some_injective τ), mul_comm]

variable {K : Type*} [Field K]
open Roth
theorem caller {l : ℕ} (D : Fin l → (τ →₀ ℕ)) (φ : Fin l → Polynomial K)
    (ψ : Fin l → MvPolynomial τ K) :
    (Matrix.of fun μ ν : Fin l ↦ hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ))
        (∑ ρ, Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) K) (φ ρ) *
          rename some (ψ ρ))).det = rename some (genWronskian D ψ) *
      Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) K) (wronskian φ) := by
  rw [genWronskian, wronskian, AlgHom.map_det, AlgHom.map_det,
    ← Matrix.det_transpose ((Polynomial.aeval (X none)).mapMatrix (wronskianMatrix φ)),
    ← Matrix.det_mul]
  congr 1
  refine Matrix.ext fun μ ν ↦ ?_
  rw [Matrix.of_apply, map_sum, Matrix.mul_apply]
  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun ρ _ ↦ ?_
  rw [mul_comm (Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) K) (φ ρ)),
    factorwise]
  rfl

#print axioms factorwise
#print axioms caller
end HasseSeparation160

namespace HasseSeparation160
open MvPolynomial
variable {σ τ R : Type*} [CommSemiring R]
theorem freshCoordinate (f : τ → σ) (hf : Function.Injective f) (i : σ)
    (hi : ∀ j, f j ≠ i) (d : τ →₀ ℕ) (ν : ℕ) (U : MvPolynomial τ R)
    (V : Polynomial R) :
    hasseDeriv (d.mapDomain f + Finsupp.single i ν)
      (rename f U * Polynomial.aeval (X i : MvPolynomial σ R) V) =
      rename f (hasseDeriv d U) *
        Polynomial.aeval (X i : MvPolynomial σ R) (Polynomial.hasseDeriv ν V) := by
  let g : Option τ → σ := fun o ↦ Option.elim o i f
  have hg : Function.Injective g := by
    intro a b h
    cases a with
    | none =>
      cases b with
      | none => rfl
      | some b => exact False.elim (hi b h.symm)
    | some a =>
      cases b with
      | none => exact False.elim (hi a h)
      | some b => exact congrArg some (hf h)
  have h := congrArg (rename g) (factorwise d ν U V)
  rw [← hasseDeriv_rename hg] at h
  simpa only [map_mul, rename_rename, rename_polynomial_aeval_X,
    Finsupp.mapDomain_add, ← Finsupp.mapDomain_comp, Finsupp.mapDomain_single,
    Function.comp_def, g, Option.elim_none, Option.elim_some] using h
#print axioms freshCoordinate
end HasseSeparation160

namespace HasseSeparation160
open MvPolynomial
variable {τ R : Type*} [CommSemiring R]
theorem originalFormAgain (d : τ →₀ ℕ) (ν : ℕ) (U : MvPolynomial τ R) (V : Polynomial R) :
    hasseDeriv (d.mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none ν) (rename some U * Polynomial.aeval
      (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) V) = rename some (hasseDeriv d U) * Polynomial.aeval
      (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) (Polynomial.hasseDeriv ν V) :=
  freshCoordinate some (Option.some_injective τ) none (fun j ↦ Option.some_ne_none j) d ν U V
#print axioms originalFormAgain
end HasseSeparation160

namespace HasseSeparation160
open MvPolynomial Roth
variable {τ K : Type*} [Field K]
theorem generalizedCaller {l : ℕ} (D : Fin l → (τ →₀ ℕ)) (φ : Fin l → Polynomial K)
    (ψ : Fin l → MvPolynomial τ K) :
    (Matrix.of fun μ ν : Fin l ↦ hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ))
        (∑ ρ, Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) K) (φ ρ) *
          rename some (ψ ρ))).det = rename some (genWronskian D ψ) *
      Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) K) (wronskian φ) := by
  rw [genWronskian, wronskian, AlgHom.map_det, AlgHom.map_det,
    ← Matrix.det_transpose ((Polynomial.aeval (X none)).mapMatrix (wronskianMatrix φ)),
    ← Matrix.det_mul]
  congr 1
  refine Matrix.ext fun μ ν ↦ ?_
  rw [Matrix.of_apply, map_sum, Matrix.mul_apply]
  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun ρ _ ↦ ?_
  rw [mul_comm (Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) K) (φ ρ)),
    freshCoordinate some (Option.some_injective τ) none (fun j ↦ Option.some_ne_none j)]
  rfl

#print axioms generalizedCaller
end HasseSeparation160

    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.roth_product_bound_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.roth_log_bound_aux,
    `Roth.roth_of_isIntegral,
    `Roth.roth,
    `Roth.roth_rat,
    `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.IsDiophantine,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.LeanEval
      `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.exists_pos_div_rpow_lt_aux,
    `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.isDiophantine_iff,
    `LeanEval.NumberTheory.ThueSiegelRothProblem.thueSiegelRoth]
  let unique := names.toList.eraseDups.length
  unless names.size == 293 && unique == 293 do
    throwError "UNIQUE_COEFF161_BAD_CANONICAL_ARRAY"
  let mut present : Nat := 0
  let mut values : Nat := 0
  for n in names do
    match env.find? n with
    | none => throwError "UNIQUE_COEFF161_NOT_LOADED: {n}"
    | some ci =>
      present := present + 1
      match ci.value? (allowOpaque := true) with
      | none => pure ()
      | some val =>
        values := values + 1
        if val.getUsedConstants.any (fun c ↦ c == target) then
          Lean.logInfo m!"UNIQUE_COEFF161_DIRECT_CONSUMER: {n}"
  Lean.logInfo m!"UNIQUE_COEFF161_AUDIT: requested={names.size}; unique={unique}"
  Lean.logInfo m!"UNIQUE_COEFF161_AUDIT_PRESENT: present={present}; values={values}"
end UniqueCoeffAudit161

namespace UniqueCoeff161Context
theorem thetaBound (V : Polynomial ℤ) (M : ℝ)
    (hVc : ∀ b, |(V.coeff b : ℝ)| ≤ M) (d : Unit →₀ ℕ) :
    |((((MvPolynomial.uniqueAlgEquiv ℤ Unit).symm V).coeff d : ℤ) : ℝ)| ≤ M := by
  rw [MvPolynomial.coeff_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm]
  exact hVc _
#print axioms thetaBound
end UniqueCoeff161Context

  Lean.logInfo m!"UNIQUE_DEGREE162_AUDIT_PRESENT: present={present}; values={values}"
end UniqueDegreeAudit162

namespace UniqueDegree162Context
open Roth MvPolynomial
section Bridge
variable {R : Type*} [CommSemiring R] {σ : Type*} [Unique σ]
theorem representationBridge (f : Polynomial R) :
    optionEquivLeft R {b : σ // b ≠ default}
      (rename (Equiv.optionSubtypeNe (default : σ)).symm ((uniqueAlgEquiv R σ).symm f)) =
        f.map MvPolynomial.C := by
  classical
  have h : (optionEquivLeft R {b : σ // b ≠ default}).toAlgHom.comp
      ((rename (Equiv.optionSubtypeNe (default : σ)).symm).comp
        (uniqueAlgEquiv R σ).symm.toAlgHom) =
      Polynomial.mapAlgHom (Algebra.ofId R _ : R →ₐ[R] MvPolynomial {b : σ // b ≠ default} R) := by
    apply Polynomial.algHom_ext
    simp [uniqueAlgEquiv_symm_apply]
  exact AlgHom.congr_fun h f
theorem canonicalRoute (f : Polynomial R) :
    degreeOf default ((uniqueAlgEquiv R σ).symm f) = f.natDegree := by
  classical
  rw [degreeOf_eq_natDegree, representationBridge]
  exact Polynomial.natDegree_map_eq_of_injective (MvPolynomial.C_injective _ _) f
end Bridge
section Domain
variable {R τ : Type*} [CommRing R] [IsDomain R]
theorem degreeOf_none_rename_mul_aeval {U : MvPolynomial τ R} (hU : U ≠ 0) (V : Polynomial R) :
    degreeOf none (rename some U * Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) V) =
      V.natDegree := by
  classical
  by_cases hV : V = 0
  · simp [hV]
  rw [degreeOf_mul_eq ((rename_eq_zero_iff_of_injective U (Option.some_injective τ)).not.mpr hU)
      ((map_ne_zero_iff (Polynomial.aeval (X none))
        (Polynomial.toMvPolynomial_injective none)).2 hV),
    (show degreeOf none (rename some U) = 0 by
      simp [degreeOf, degrees_rename_of_injective (Option.some_injective τ)]),
    zero_add, aeval_X_eq_rename]
  exact (degreeOf_rename_of_injective (f := fun _ : Unit ↦ (none : Option τ))
    (fun _ _ _ ↦ rfl) ()).trans (canonicalRoute V)

end Domain
section Theta
variable {τ : Type*} [Fintype τ]
theorem thetaCaller {r : Option τ → ℕ} (hr0 : ∀ j, 0 < r j) {q : Option τ → ℕ}
    {h : Option τ → ℤ} (hh : ∀ j, IsCoprime (h j) (q j : ℤ)) {M : ℝ} {l : ℕ} (hl : 0 < l)
    {U : MvPolynomial τ ℤ} (hU : U ∈ RSet M (fun j ↦ l * r (some j))) {V : Polynomial ℤ}
    (hV : V ≠ 0) (hVd : V.natDegree ≤ l * r none) (hVc : ∀ b, |(V.coeff b : ℝ)| ≤ M) :
    index (map (Int.castRingHom ℝ)
        (rename some U * Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℤ) V))
      (fun j ↦ (h j : ℝ) / q j) r ≤ l * lem6Φ q r M l := by
  classical
  set V' : MvPolynomial Unit ℤ := (uniqueAlgEquiv ℤ Unit).symm V with hV'
  have hV'0 : V' ≠ 0 := by
    rwa [hV', ne_eq, map_eq_zero_iff _ (uniqueAlgEquiv ℤ Unit).symm.injective]
  have hVmem : V' ∈ RSet M (fun _ : Unit ↦ l * r none) := by
    refine ⟨hV'0, fun j ↦ ?_, fun d ↦ ?_⟩
    · rw [Subsingleton.elim j default, hV', canonicalRoute]
      exact hVd
    · rw [hV', MvPolynomial.coeff_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm]
      exact hVc _
  have hinjV : Function.Injective (fun _ : Unit ↦ (none : Option τ)) :=
    fun a b _ ↦ Subsingleton.elim a b
  have hmU : map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) U ≠ 0 := fun h0 ↦
    hU.1 (map_injective _ Int.cast_injective (h0.trans (map_zero _).symm))
  have hmV : map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) V' ≠ 0 := fun h0 ↦
    hV'0 (map_injective _ Int.cast_injective (h0.trans (map_zero _).symm))
  rw [aeval_X_eq_rename, _root_.map_mul, map_rename, map_rename,
    index_mul ((map_ne_zero_iff (rename (R := ℝ) some)
      (rename_injective _ (Option.some_injective _))).2 hmU)
      ((map_ne_zero_iff (rename (R := ℝ) (fun _ : Unit ↦ (none : Option τ)))
        (rename_injective _ hinjV)).2 hmV) _ hr0,
    index_rename (Option.some_injective _), index_rename hinjV]
  have hUΘ := index_le_Θ hU (h := h ∘ some)
    fun j ↦ hh (some j)
  have hVΘ := index_le_Θ hVmem
    (q := fun _ : Unit ↦ q none) (h := fun _ ↦ h none) fun _ ↦ hh none
  rw [index_mul_left_weights] at hUΘ hVΘ
  have hlR : (0 : ℝ) < l := by exact_mod_cast hl
  rw [lem6Φ, mul_add, add_comm]
  exact add_le_add ((div_le_iff₀' hlR).1 hVΘ) ((div_le_iff₀' hlR).1 hUΘ)

end Theta
#print axioms representationBridge
#print axioms canonicalRoute
#print axioms degreeOf_none_rename_mul_aeval
#print axioms thetaCaller
end UniqueDegree162Context

    throwError "SEPARATED_DEGREE163_BAD_CANONICAL_ARRAY"
  let mut present : Nat := 0
  let mut values : Nat := 0
  for n in names do
    match env.find? n with
    | none => throwError "SEPARATED_DEGREE163_NOT_LOADED: {n}"
    | some ci =>
      present := present + 1
      match ci.value? (allowOpaque := true) with
      | none => pure ()
      | some val =>
        values := values + 1
        if val.getUsedConstants.any (fun c ↦ c == target) then
          Lean.logInfo m!"SEPARATED_DEGREE163_DIRECT_CONSUMER: {n}"

  Lean.logInfo m!"SEPARATED_DEGREE163_AUDIT_PRESENT: present={present}; values={values}"

namespace SeparatedDegree163Caller

variable {τ : Type*}
elab "ratFactors163%" : term =>
  Lean.Meta.mkConstWithFreshMVarLevels
    (Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Separation `Roth.exists_rat_factors_det_hasseDeriv_aux)
theorem exists_det_eq_mul [Finite τ] {P : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℤ} (hP : P ≠ 0) {r : Option τ → ℕ}
    (hr : ∀ j, degreeOf j P ≤ r j) : ∃ l, 1 ≤ l ∧ l ≤ r none + 1 ∧ ∃ D : Fin l → (τ →₀ ℕ),
      (∀ μ, (D μ).degree ≤ μ) ∧ ∃ (U : MvPolynomial τ ℤ) (V : Polynomial ℤ), U ≠ 0 ∧ V ≠ 0 ∧
        (∀ j, degreeOf j U ≤ l * r (some j)) ∧ V.natDegree ≤ l * r none ∧
        (Matrix.of fun μ ν : Fin l ↦
            hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) P).det = rename some U *
          Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℤ) V := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨l, hl1, hl2, D, hD, U', V', hU', hV', hFmap⟩ :=
    ratFactors163% hP (hr none)
  obtain ⟨U, V, hU, hV, hFUV, -, -⟩ := exists_int_factors hU' hV' hFmap
  refine ⟨l, hl1, hl2, D, hD, U, V, hU, hV, fun j ↦ ?_, ?_, hFUV⟩
  · have hdegree : degreeOf (some j)
        (rename some U * Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℤ) V) =
          degreeOf j U := by
      rw [degreeOf_mul_eq
        ((rename_eq_zero_iff_of_injective U (Option.some_injective τ)).not.mpr hU)
        ((map_ne_zero_iff (Polynomial.aeval (X none))
          (Polynomial.toMvPolynomial_injective none)).2 hV),
        degreeOf_rename_of_injective (Option.some_injective τ)]
      rw [show Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℤ) V =
          rename (fun _ : Unit ↦ none) ((uniqueAlgEquiv ℤ Unit).symm V) from
            AlgHom.congr_fun (Polynomial.toMvPolynomial_eq_rename_comp none) V]
      simp [degreeOf, degrees_rename_of_injective (Function.injective_of_subsingleton _),
        Multiset.mem_replicate]
    rw [← hdegree, ← hFUV]
    exact degreeOf_det_hasseDeriv_le
      (fun μ ν : Fin l ↦ (D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) P hr (some j)
  · rw [← degreeOf_none_rename_mul_aeval hU V, ← hFUV]
    exact degreeOf_det_hasseDeriv_le
      (fun μ ν : Fin l ↦ (D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) P hr none

#print axioms exists_det_eq_mul
end SeparatedDegree163Caller

  for n in names do
    match env.find? n with
    | none => throwError "NONE_DEGREE164_NOT_LOADED: {n}"
    | some ci =>
      present := present + 1
      match ci.value? (allowOpaque := true) with
      | none => pure ()
      | some val =>
        values := values + 1
        if val.getUsedConstants.any (fun c ↦ c == target) then
          Lean.logInfo m!"NONE_DEGREE164_DIRECT_CONSUMER: {n}"

  Lean.logInfo m!"NONE_DEGREE164_AUDIT_PRESENT: present={present}; values={values}"

namespace NoneDegree164Callers
open Roth MvPolynomial
variable {τ : Type*}
elab "ratFactors164%" : term =>
  Lean.Meta.mkConstWithFreshMVarLevels
    (Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Separation `Roth.exists_rat_factors_det_hasseDeriv_aux)
theorem exists_det_eq_mul [Finite τ] {P : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℤ} (hP : P ≠ 0) {r : Option τ → ℕ}
    (hr : ∀ j, degreeOf j P ≤ r j) : ∃ l, 1 ≤ l ∧ l ≤ r none + 1 ∧ ∃ D : Fin l → (τ →₀ ℕ),
      (∀ μ, (D μ).degree ≤ μ) ∧ ∃ (U : MvPolynomial τ ℤ) (V : Polynomial ℤ), U ≠ 0 ∧ V ≠ 0 ∧
        (∀ j, degreeOf j U ≤ l * r (some j)) ∧ V.natDegree ≤ l * r none ∧
        (Matrix.of fun μ ν : Fin l ↦
            hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) P).det = rename some U *
          Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℤ) V := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨l, hl1, hl2, D, hD, U', V', hU', hV', hFmap⟩ :=
    ratFactors164% hP (hr none)
  obtain ⟨U, V, hU, hV, hFUV, -, -⟩ := exists_int_factors hU' hV' hFmap
  refine ⟨l, hl1, hl2, D, hD, U, V, hU, hV, fun j ↦ ?_, ?_, hFUV⟩
  · rw [← degreeOf_rename_mul_aeval U hV j, ← hFUV]
    exact degreeOf_det_hasseDeriv_le
      (fun μ ν : Fin l ↦ (D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) P hr (some j)
  · have hdegree : degreeOf none
        (rename some U * Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℤ) V) =
          V.natDegree := by
      have h : (optionEquivLeft ℤ τ).toAlgHom.comp (rename some) = Polynomial.CAlgHom := by
        apply MvPolynomial.algHom_ext
        intro i
        simp
      rw [← natDegree_optionEquivLeft, map_mul,
        (show optionEquivLeft ℤ τ (rename some U) = Polynomial.C U from AlgHom.congr_fun h U),
        ← Polynomial.aeval_algHom_apply, optionEquivLeft_X_none,
        Polynomial.aeval_X_left_eq_map, Polynomial.natDegree_C_mul hU]
      exact Polynomial.natDegree_map_eq_of_injective (MvPolynomial.C_injective _ _) V
    rw [← hdegree, ← hFUV]
    exact degreeOf_det_hasseDeriv_le
      (fun μ ν : Fin l ↦ (D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) P hr none

#print axioms exists_det_eq_mul
end NoneDegree164Callers

namespace SeparationPrivate165

variable {τ : Type*} [Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ]

section General

variable {R : Type*} [CommSemiring R]

omit [Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ] in
/-- Every multi-index on `Option τ` is `(its restriction to τ) + (d none) e_none`. -/
theorem eq_mapDomain_some_add_single (d : Option τ →₀ ℕ) :
    d = (Finsupp.some d).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (d none) := by
  ext o
  cases o <;> simp [Finsupp.mapDomain_of_notMem_range,
    Finsupp.mapDomain_apply_of_injective (Option.some_injective τ), Finsupp.some_apply]

omit [Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ] in
private lemma coeff_X_none_pow_mul_rename (ν : ℕ) (Q : MvPolynomial τ R) (e : Option τ →₀ ℕ) :
    (X none ^ ν * rename some Q).coeff e = if e none = ν then Q.coeff (Finsupp.some e) else 0 := by
  classical
  have h : (optionEquivLeft R τ).toAlgHom.comp (rename some) = Polynomial.CAlgHom := by
    ext i
    simp
  have hQ : optionEquivLeft R τ (rename some Q) = Polynomial.C Q := AlgHom.congr_fun h Q
  rw [← optionEquivLeft_coeff_some_coeff_none R τ e, map_mul, map_pow,
    optionEquivLeft_X_none, hQ, Polynomial.X_pow_mul_C,
    Polynomial.C_mul_X_pow_eq_monomial, Polynomial.coeff_monomial]
  by_cases he : e none = ν
  · simp [he]
  · simp [he, Ne.symm he]

/-- The coefficient of `X none ^ ν` in `P`, a polynomial in the variables `τ`.
Source: §2, proof of Lemma 2 ("collecting the terms in terms of the powers of `x_p`"). -/
noncomputable def coeffNone (P : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) (ν : ℕ) : MvPolynomial τ R :=
  (optionEquivLeft R τ P).coeff ν

omit [Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ] in
/-- The coefficient of `x_τ^d` in `coeffNone P ν` is the coefficient of `x_τ^d x_none^ν` in `P`. -/
theorem coeff_coeffNone (P : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) (ν : ℕ) (d : τ →₀ ℕ) :
    (coeffNone P ν).coeff d = P.coeff (d.mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none ν) := by
  rw [coeffNone, optionEquivLeft_coeff_coeff]
  congr 1
  ext o
  cases o <;> simp [Finsupp.mapDomain_of_notMem_range,
    Finsupp.mapDomain_apply_of_injective (Option.some_injective τ)]

omit [Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ] in
/-- Expansion of `P` in powers of the distinguished variable: if `P` has degree at most `n` in
`x_none`, then `P = ∑_{ν ≤ n} x_none^ν · coeffNone P ν`.
Source: §2, proof of Lemma 2 ("we can let `l - 1 = r_p` and `φ_ν(x_p) = x_p^ν` by collecting
the terms in terms of the powers of `x_p`"). -/
theorem eq_sum_X_none_pow_mul_coeffNone (P : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) {n : ℕ}
    (hn : degreeOf none P ≤ n) :
    P = ∑ ν ∈ Finset.range (n + 1), X none ^ ν * rename some (coeffNone P ν) := by
  classical
  ext e
  rw [coeff_sum]
  simp_rw [coeff_X_none_pow_mul_rename]
  rw [Finset.sum_ite_eq]
  split_ifs with he
  · rw [coeff_coeffNone, ← eq_mapDomain_some_add_single]
  · exact notMem_support_iff.1 fun h ↦
      he (Finset.mem_range.2 (Nat.lt_succ_of_le ((monomial_le_degreeOf none h).trans hn)))

omit [Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ] in
/-- Taking the coefficient of a power of `x_none` does not increase the degree in `x_j`.
Source: §2, proof of Lemma 2 ("the `ψ_ν`'s of degree at most `r_j` in `x_j`"). -/
theorem degreeOf_coeffNone_le (P : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) (ν : ℕ) (j : τ) :
    degreeOf j (coeffNone P ν) ≤ degreeOf (some j) P := by
  refine degreeOf_le_iff.2 fun d hd ↦ ?_
  simpa using monomial_le_degreeOf (some j) ((mem_support_coeff_optionEquivLeft R).1 hd)

omit [Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ] in
/-- The coefficient of `x_τ^a x_none^b` in `U(x_τ) V(x_none)` is `U_a V_b`.
Source: §2, proof of Lemma 2 ("the variables of the two factors are disjoint": the
coefficients of `U(x_τ) V(x_none)` are the products of coefficients). -/
theorem coeff_rename_mul_aeval (U : MvPolynomial τ R) (V : Polynomial R) (a : τ →₀ ℕ) (b : ℕ) :
    (rename some U * Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) V).coeff
        (a.mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none b) = U.coeff a * V.coeff b := by
  have h : (optionEquivLeft R τ).toAlgHom.comp (rename some) = Polynomial.CAlgHom := by
    ext i
    simp
  rw [← coeff_coeffNone, coeffNone, map_mul,
    (show optionEquivLeft R τ (rename some U) = Polynomial.C U from AlgHom.congr_fun h U),
    ← Polynomial.aeval_algHom_apply, optionEquivLeft_X_none, Polynomial.aeval_X_left_eq_map,
    Polynomial.coeff_C_mul, Polynomial.coeff_map, algebraMap_eq, mul_comm U, coeff_C_mul, mul_comm]

/-- A univariate polynomial in the variable `X i`, as the renaming of a one-variable
`MvPolynomial`. Source: §4, Lemma 4-2 ("the index of the embedded `Q` is its one-variable
index"). -/
theorem aeval_X_eq_rename {σ : Type*} (i : σ) (f : Polynomial R) :
    Polynomial.aeval (X i : MvPolynomial σ R) f =
      rename (fun _ : Unit ↦ i) ((uniqueAlgEquiv R Unit).symm f) :=
  AlgHom.congr_fun (Polynomial.toMvPolynomial_eq_rename_comp i) f

omit [Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ] in
/-- A Hasse derivative `Δ_{d + ν e_none}` of a separated product `U(x_τ) V(x_none)` acts
factorwise. Source: §2, proof of Lemma 2 ("`Δ_μ (1/ν!) ∂^ν_{x_p} (φ_ρ(x_p) ψ_ρ(x_1, …, x_{p-1}))
= (Δ_μ ψ_ρ) ((1/ν!) ∂^ν φ_ρ)`", the entries of `A Bᵀ`). -/
theorem hasseDeriv_rename_mul_aeval (d : τ →₀ ℕ) (ν : ℕ) (U : MvPolynomial τ R) (V : Polynomial R) :
    hasseDeriv (d.mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none ν) (rename some U * Polynomial.aeval
      (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) V) = rename some (hasseDeriv d U) * Polynomial.aeval
      (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) (Polynomial.hasseDeriv ν V) := by
  classical
  have hnone : ∀ {o : Option τ}, o ∈ (d.mapDomain some).support → o ≠ none := by
    simp
  have hdisj : Disjoint (d.mapDomain some).support (Finsupp.single none ν).support :=
    Finset.disjoint_left.2 fun _ ho ho' ↦
      hnone ho (Finset.mem_singleton.1 (Finsupp.support_single_subset ho'))
  rw [← hasseDeriv_hasseDeriv_of_disjoint hdisj,
    hasseDeriv_mul_of_degreeOf_eq_zero fun j hj ↦ by
      rw [Finset.mem_singleton.1 (Finsupp.support_single_subset hj)]
      simp [degreeOf, degrees_rename_of_injective (Option.some_injective τ)],
    hasseDeriv_single_aeval_X, mul_comm,
    hasseDeriv_mul_of_degreeOf_eq_zero fun j hj ↦ by
      simp [aeval_X_eq_rename, degreeOf,
        degrees_rename_of_injective (Function.injective_of_subsingleton _), Multiset.mem_replicate,
        hnone hj],
    hasseDeriv_rename (Option.some_injective τ), mul_comm]

/-- Source: §5, proof of Lemma 6 ("`V(x_p) ∈ ℛ_1(M; l r_p)`"): the coefficients of a univariate
polynomial viewed as a one-variable `MvPolynomial`. -/
theorem coeff_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm {σ : Type*} [Unique σ] (f : Polynomial R) (n : ℕ) :
    ((uniqueAlgEquiv R σ).symm f).coeff (Finsupp.single default n) = f.coeff n := by
  rw [MvPolynomial.coeff_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm, Finsupp.single_eq_same]

/-- The degree of a univariate polynomial viewed as a one-variable `MvPolynomial`. -/
theorem degreeOf_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm {σ : Type*} [Unique σ] (f : Polynomial R) :
    degreeOf default ((uniqueAlgEquiv R σ).symm f) = f.natDegree := by
  apply le_antisymm
  · rw [degreeOf_le_iff]
    intro e he
    rw [mem_support_iff, MvPolynomial.coeff_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm] at he
    exact Polynomial.le_natDegree_of_ne_zero he
  · refine Polynomial.natDegree_le_iff_coeff_eq_zero.2 fun n hn ↦ ?_
    simpa only [mem_support_iff, MvPolynomial.coeff_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm,
      Finsupp.single_eq_same, not_not] using
      (notMem_support_of_degreeOf_lt (p := (uniqueAlgEquiv R σ).symm f) (i := default)
        (s := Finsupp.single default n) (by simpa only [Finsupp.single_eq_same] using hn))

end General

section Domain

variable {R : Type*} [CommRing R] [IsDomain R]

omit [Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ] in
/-- For `V ≠ 0`, the degree of `U(x_τ) V(x_none)` in `x_j` is the degree of `U` in `x_j`.
Source: §2, end of the proof of Lemma 2 ("since the variables are disjoint and both factors are
nonzero, the individual degrees of `U, V` equal the corresponding degrees of `F`"). -/
theorem degreeOf_rename_mul_aeval (U : MvPolynomial τ R) {V : Polynomial R} (hV : V ≠ 0) (j : τ) :
    degreeOf (some j) (rename some U * Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) V) =
      degreeOf j U := by
  classical
  by_cases hU : U = 0
  · simp [hU]
  rw [degreeOf_mul_eq ((rename_eq_zero_iff_of_injective U (Option.some_injective τ)).not.mpr hU)
      ((map_ne_zero_iff (Polynomial.aeval (X none))
        (Polynomial.toMvPolynomial_injective none)).2 hV),
    degreeOf_rename_of_injective (Option.some_injective τ)]
  simp [aeval_X_eq_rename, degreeOf,
    degrees_rename_of_injective (Function.injective_of_subsingleton _), Multiset.mem_replicate]

omit [Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ] in
/-- For `U ≠ 0`, the degree of `U(x_τ) V(x_none)` in `x_none` is the degree of `V`.
Source: §2, end of the proof of Lemma 2. -/
theorem degreeOf_none_rename_mul_aeval {U : MvPolynomial τ R} (hU : U ≠ 0) (V : Polynomial R) :
    degreeOf none (rename some U * Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) V) =
      V.natDegree := by
  classical
  by_cases hV : V = 0
  · simp [hV]
  rw [degreeOf_mul_eq ((rename_eq_zero_iff_of_injective U (Option.some_injective τ)).not.mpr hU)
      ((map_ne_zero_iff (Polynomial.aeval (X none))
        (Polynomial.toMvPolynomial_injective none)).2 hV),
    (show degreeOf none (rename some U) = 0 by
      simp [degreeOf, degrees_rename_of_injective (Option.some_injective τ)]),
    zero_add, aeval_X_eq_rename]
  exact (degreeOf_rename_of_injective (f := fun _ : Unit ↦ (none : Option τ))
    (fun _ _ _ ↦ rfl) ()).trans (degreeOf_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm V)

end Domain

section Field

variable {K : Type*} [Field K]

omit [Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ] in
theorem replay {R M : Type*} [CommRing R]
    [AddCommMonoid M] [Module R M] {n l : ℕ} (v : Fin (n + 1) → M)
    (b : Module.Basis (Fin l) R (Submodule.span R (Set.range v))) :
    LinearIndependent R (fun ρ ↦ ∑ ν : Fin (n + 1),
      Polynomial.C (b.repr ⟨v ν, Submodule.subset_span ⟨ν, rfl⟩⟩ ρ) * Polynomial.X ^ (ν : ℕ)) := by
  classical
  let a := fun ν ρ ↦ b.repr ⟨v ν, Submodule.subset_span ⟨ν, rfl⟩⟩ ρ
  rw [Fintype.linearIndependent_iff]
  intro g hg ρ₀
  have hcoeff : ∀ ν : Fin (n + 1), ∑ ρ, g ρ * a ν ρ = 0 := by
    intro ν
    have h1 := congrArg (fun p : Polynomial R ↦ p.coeff ν) hg
    simp only [Polynomial.finsetSum_coeff, Polynomial.coeff_smul, smul_eq_mul,
      Polynomial.coeff_C_mul_X_pow, Polynomial.coeff_zero] at h1
    rw [← h1]
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun ρ _ ↦ ?_
    rw [Finset.sum_eq_single ν (fun ν' _ hν' ↦ ite_eq_right fun h ↦ hν' (Fin.ext h).symm)
      (fun h ↦ absurd (Finset.mem_univ ν) h), ite_eq_left rfl]
  let f : Submodule.span R (Set.range v) →ₗ[R] R := ∑ ρ, g ρ • b.coord ρ
  have hf : f = 0 := LinearMap.ext_on Submodule.span_span_coe_preimage fun x hx ↦ by
    obtain ⟨ν, hν⟩ := hx
    obtain rfl : x = ⟨v ν, Submodule.subset_span ⟨ν, rfl⟩⟩ := Subtype.ext hν.symm
    simpa only [f, a, LinearMap.sum_apply, LinearMap.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul,
      Module.Basis.coord_apply, LinearMap.zero_apply] using hcoeff ν
  simpa only [f, LinearMap.sum_apply, LinearMap.smul_apply, Module.Basis.coord_apply,
    Module.Basis.repr_self, Finsupp.single_apply, smul_eq_mul, mul_ite, mul_one, mul_zero,
    Finset.sum_ite_eq, Finset.mem_univ, ite_true, LinearMap.zero_apply] using
    LinearMap.congr_fun hf (b ρ₀)

omit [Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ] in
private lemma eq_sum_aeval_basis_coeffNone_aux {R : Type*} [CommSemiring R]
    {P : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R} {n l : ℕ}
    (hP : P = ∑ ν : Fin (n + 1), X none ^ (ν : ℕ) * rename some (coeffNone P ν))
    (b : Module.Basis (Fin l) R
      (Submodule.span R (Set.range (fun ν : Fin (n + 1) ↦ coeffNone P ν)))) :
    P = ∑ ρ, Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) (∑ ν : Fin (n + 1),
        Polynomial.C (b.repr ⟨coeffNone P ν, Submodule.subset_span ⟨ν, rfl⟩⟩ ρ) *
          Polynomial.X ^ (ν : ℕ)) * rename some (b ρ : MvPolynomial τ R) := by
  let a := fun (ν : Fin (n + 1)) ρ ↦ b.repr ⟨coeffNone P ν, Submodule.subset_span ⟨ν, rfl⟩⟩ ρ
  have hexp : ∀ ν : Fin (n + 1), coeffNone P ν = ∑ ρ, a ν ρ • (b ρ : MvPolynomial τ R) := fun ν ↦ by
    have h := congrArg Subtype.val (b.sum_repr ⟨coeffNone P ν, Submodule.subset_span ⟨ν, rfl⟩⟩)
    simpa only [Submodule.coe_sum, Submodule.coe_smul] using h.symm
  change P = ∑ ρ, Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R)
    (∑ ν : Fin (n + 1), Polynomial.C (a ν ρ) * Polynomial.X ^ (ν : ℕ)) *
      rename some (b ρ : MvPolynomial τ R)
  conv_lhs => rw [hP]
  simp_rw [hexp, map_sum, Finset.mul_sum]
  rw [Finset.sum_comm]
  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun ρ _ ↦ ?_
  rw [Finset.sum_mul]
  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun ν _ ↦ ?_
  rw [map_smul, smul_eq_C_mul, map_mul, Polynomial.aeval_C, algebraMap_eq, map_pow,
    Polynomial.aeval_X]
  ac_rfl

omit [Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ] in
private lemma exists_coeffNone_basis_aux {P : MvPolynomial (Option τ) K} (hP : P ≠ 0) {n : ℕ}
    (hP' : P = ∑ ν : Fin (n + 1), X none ^ (ν : ℕ) * rename some (coeffNone P ν)) :
    ∃ l, ∃ b : Module.Basis (Fin l) K
      (Submodule.span K (Set.range (fun ν : Fin (n + 1) ↦ coeffNone P ν))),
      1 ≤ l ∧ l ≤ n + 1 ∧ ∀ ρ j, degreeOf j (b ρ : MvPolynomial τ K) ≤ degreeOf (some j) P := by
  classical
  let v : Fin (n + 1) → MvPolynomial τ K := fun ν ↦ coeffNone P ν
  let V := Submodule.span K (Set.range v)
  have : FiniteDimensional K V := FiniteDimensional.span_of_finite K (Set.finite_range v)
  let b := Module.finBasis K V
  have hl1 : 1 ≤ Module.finrank K V := by
    obtain ⟨ν, hν⟩ : ∃ ν, v ν ≠ 0 := by
      by_contra! h
      apply hP
      change P = ∑ ν : Fin (n + 1), X none ^ (ν : ℕ) * rename some (v ν) at hP'
      rw [hP']
      simp [h]
    exact Module.finrank_pos_iff_exists_ne_zero.2
      ⟨⟨v ν, Submodule.subset_span ⟨ν, rfl⟩⟩, fun h ↦ hν (congrArg Subtype.val h)⟩
  have hdegree : V ≤ restrictSupport K {d | ∀ j, d j ≤ degreeOf (some j) P} :=
    Submodule.span_le.2 (Set.range_subset_iff.2 fun (ν : Fin (n + 1)) d hd j ↦ by
      simpa using monomial_le_degreeOf (some j)
        ((mem_support_coeff_optionEquivLeft K).1 hd))
  exact ⟨Module.finrank K V, b, hl1,
    (finrank_range_le_card (R := K) v).trans_eq (Fintype.card_fin _),
    fun ρ j ↦ degreeOf_le_iff.2 fun d hd ↦ hdegree (b ρ).2 hd j⟩

omit [Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ] in
/-- Over a field, a nonzero `P` of degree at most `n` in `x_none` can be written as
`∑_{ν < l} φ_ν(x_none) ψ_ν(x_τ)` with `1 ≤ l ≤ n + 1`, both families linearly independent,
`deg φ_ν ≤ n`, and each `ψ_ν` of degree in `x_j` at most that of `P`.
Source: §2, proof of Lemma 2 and its Lean implementation note ("Construct the separated
decomposition over `ℚ` from a basis of the span of the coefficient polynomials of `R` in `x_p`";
the `φ_ν` and `ψ_ν` are linearly independent). -/
theorem exists_separated_representation {P : MvPolynomial (Option τ) K} (hP : P ≠ 0) {n : ℕ}
    (hn : degreeOf none P ≤ n) :
    ∃ l, 1 ≤ l ∧ l ≤ n + 1 ∧ ∃ (φ : Fin l → Polynomial K) (ψ : Fin l → MvPolynomial τ K),
      LinearIndependent K φ ∧ LinearIndependent K ψ ∧ (∀ ν, (φ ν).natDegree ≤ n) ∧
      (∀ ν j, degreeOf j (ψ ν) ≤ degreeOf (some j) P) ∧
      P = ∑ ν, Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) K) (φ ν) * rename some (ψ ν) := by
  classical
  let v : Fin (n + 1) → MvPolynomial τ K := fun ν ↦ coeffNone P ν
  have hP' : P = ∑ ν : Fin (n + 1), X none ^ (ν : ℕ) * rename some (v ν) := by
    rw [Fin.sum_univ_eq_sum_range (fun ν ↦ X none ^ ν * rename some (coeffNone P ν))]
    exact eq_sum_X_none_pow_mul_coeffNone P hn
  obtain ⟨l, b, hl1, hl2, hdegree⟩ := exists_coeffNone_basis_aux hP hP'
  refine ⟨l, hl1, hl2, fun ρ ↦ ∑ ν : Fin (n + 1),
      Polynomial.C (b.repr ⟨v ν, Submodule.subset_span ⟨ν, rfl⟩⟩ ρ) * Polynomial.X ^ (ν : ℕ),
    fun ρ ↦ (b ρ : MvPolynomial τ K), replay v b,
    b.linearIndependent.map' (Submodule.span K (Set.range v)).subtype
      (Submodule.ker_subtype _), ?_, hdegree,
    eq_sum_aeval_basis_coeffNone_aux hP' b⟩
  exact fun ρ ↦ Polynomial.natDegree_sum_le_of_forall_le _ _ fun ν _ ↦
    (Polynomial.natDegree_C_mul_X_pow_le _ _).trans (Nat.lt_succ_iff.1 ν.2)

omit [Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ] in
/-- The determinant of Hasse derivatives of a separated sum `∑ φ_ρ(x_none) ψ_ρ(x_τ)` is the
product of the generalised Wronskian of `ψ` and the Wronskian of `φ`.
Source: §2, proof of Lemma 2 ("The matrix defining `F` is `A Bᵀ`, so `F = det A det B` by
multiplicativity and invariance under transpose. Explicitly, we obtain `GW = det (...)`"). -/
theorem det_hasseDeriv_separated {l : ℕ} (D : Fin l → (τ →₀ ℕ)) (φ : Fin l → Polynomial K)
    (ψ : Fin l → MvPolynomial τ K) :
    (Matrix.of fun μ ν : Fin l ↦ hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ))
        (∑ ρ, Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) K) (φ ρ) *
          rename some (ψ ρ))).det = rename some (genWronskian D ψ) *
      Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) K) (wronskian φ) := by
  rw [genWronskian, wronskian, AlgHom.map_det, AlgHom.map_det,
    ← Matrix.det_transpose ((Polynomial.aeval (X none)).mapMatrix (wronskianMatrix φ)),
    ← Matrix.det_mul]
  congr 1
  refine Matrix.ext fun μ ν ↦ ?_
  rw [Matrix.of_apply, map_sum, Matrix.mul_apply]
  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun ρ _ ↦ ?_
  rw [mul_comm (Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) K) (φ ρ)),
    hasseDeriv_rename_mul_aeval]
  rfl

end Field

section Integrality

/-- Bézout's identity for a finite family of integers. Source: §2, Lean implementation note
("if integer coefficients `u_a` have gcd `1` ... by a Bézout relation"). -/
theorem exists_sum_mul_eq_gcd {ι : Type*} (s : Finset ι) (u : ι → ℤ) :
    ∃ z : ι → ℤ, ∑ i ∈ s, z i * u i = s.gcd u := by
  simpa [eq_comm, mul_comm] using Finset.gcd_eq_sum_mul s u

/-- A rational polynomial becomes integral after multiplication by a positive integer.
Source: §2, proof of Lemma 2 ("clearing denominators"). -/
theorem exists_nat_smul_eq_map {σ : Type*} (G : MvPolynomial σ ℚ) :
    ∃ (N : ℕ) (U : MvPolynomial σ ℤ), 0 < N ∧ map (Int.castRingHom ℚ) U = (N : ℚ) • G := by
  classical
  set N := ∏ d ∈ G.support, (G.coeff d).den with hN
  have hint : ∀ d, ∃ z : ℤ, (N : ℚ) * G.coeff d = z := by
    intro d
    by_cases hd : d ∈ G.support
    · obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := Finset.dvd_prod_of_mem (fun d ↦ (G.coeff d).den) hd
      refine ⟨k * (G.coeff d).num, ?_⟩
      rw [hN, hk]
      push_cast
      rw [mul_comm ((G.coeff d).den : ℚ) k, mul_assoc, Rat.den_mul_eq_num]
    · exact ⟨0, by simp [notMem_support_iff.1 hd]⟩
  choose c hc using hint
  refine ⟨N, ∑ d ∈ G.support, monomial d (c d), Finset.prod_pos fun _ _ ↦ Rat.den_pos _, ?_⟩
  ext e
  rw [coeff_map, coeff_sum, coeff_smul, smul_eq_mul, hc e]
  simp only [coeff_monomial, Finset.sum_ite_eq']
  split_ifs with he
  · simp
  · simpa [notMem_support_iff.1 he] using hc e

/-- Every nonzero rational polynomial has a nonzero rational multiple that is an integral
polynomial with coprime coefficients.
Source: §2, proof of Lemma 2 ("Choose `g ∈ ℚ×` so that `U = gG` is an integral primitive
polynomial, by clearing denominators and then dividing by the gcd of its coefficients"). -/
theorem exists_primitive_smul_eq {σ : Type*} {G : MvPolynomial σ ℚ} (hG : G ≠ 0) :
    ∃ (g : ℚ) (U : MvPolynomial σ ℤ), g ≠ 0 ∧ map (Int.castRingHom ℚ) U = g • G ∧
      U.support.gcd (fun d ↦ U.coeff d) = 1 := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨N, U0, hN, hU0⟩ := exists_nat_smul_eq_map G
  have hne : U0.support.Nonempty := support_nonempty.mpr fun h ↦ by
    simpa [h, smul_eq_zero, hN.ne', hG] using hU0.symm
  obtain ⟨g', hg', hg1⟩ := Finset.extract_gcd (fun d ↦ U0.coeff d) hne
  set g0 := U0.support.gcd (fun d ↦ U0.coeff d) with hg0def
  have hg0 : g0 ≠ 0 := by
    obtain ⟨d, hd⟩ := hne
    rw [hg0def, Ne, Finset.gcd_eq_zero_iff]
    exact fun h ↦ mem_support_iff.1 hd (h d hd)
  set U := ∑ d ∈ U0.support, monomial d (g' d) with hUdef
  have hUcoeff : ∀ d, U.coeff d = if d ∈ U0.support then g' d else 0 := by
    simp [hUdef, coeff_monomial, Finset.sum_ite_eq']
  have hU0coeff : ∀ d, U0.coeff d = g0 * U.coeff d := by
    intro d
    by_cases hd : d ∈ U0.support
    · simpa [hUcoeff, hd] using hg' d hd
    · simp [hUcoeff, hd, notMem_support_iff.1 hd]
  refine ⟨N / g0, U, div_ne_zero (by exact_mod_cast hN.ne') (by exact_mod_cast hg0), ?_, ?_⟩
  · ext d
    have h1 := congrArg (fun P ↦ P.coeff d) hU0
    simp only [coeff_map, coeff_smul, smul_eq_mul, eq_intCast] at h1 ⊢
    rw [hU0coeff] at h1
    push_cast at h1
    rwa [div_mul_eq_mul_div, eq_div_iff (by exact_mod_cast hg0), mul_comm]
  · have hsupp : U.support = U0.support := by
      ext d
      simp [mem_support_iff, hU0coeff d, hg0]
    rw [hsupp, ← hg1]
    exact Finset.gcd_congr rfl fun d hd ↦ by rw [hUcoeff, ite_eq_left hd]

/-- If integers `u_a` have gcd `1` and every `u_a v` is an integer, then `v` is an integer.
Source: §2, proof of Lemma 2 ("Primitivity gives a finite integral linear combination
`∑ z_a u_a = 1`, so `v_b = ∑ z_a (u_a v_b)` is an integer"). -/
theorem exists_int_eq_of_gcd_eq_one {ι : Type*} {s : Finset ι} {u : ι → ℤ} (hu : s.gcd u = 1)
    {v : ℚ} (hv : ∀ a ∈ s, ∃ z : ℤ, (u a : ℚ) * v = z) : ∃ z : ℤ, v = z := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨z, hz⟩ := exists_sum_mul_eq_gcd s u
  rw [hu] at hz
  choose! w hw using hv
  refine ⟨∑ i ∈ s, z i * w i, ?_⟩
  calc v = ((∑ i ∈ s, z i * u i : ℤ) : ℚ) * v := by simp [hz]
    _ = ((∑ i ∈ s, z i * w i : ℤ) : ℚ) := by
        push_cast
        rw [Finset.sum_mul]
        exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i hi ↦ by rw [mul_assoc, hw i hi]

omit [Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ] in
private lemma rescale_separated_factor_aux {K : Type*} [Field K] {U' U : MvPolynomial τ K}
    {V' : Polynomial K} {F : MvPolynomial (Option τ) K} {g : K} (hg : g ≠ 0) (hUmap : U = g • U')
    (hF : F = rename some U' * Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) K) V') :
    F = rename some U * Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) K)
      (Polynomial.C g⁻¹ * V') := by
  rw [hF, hUmap, map_mul, Polynomial.aeval_C, algebraMap_eq, smul_eq_C_mul, map_mul,
    rename_C, mul_mul_mul_comm, ← C_mul, mul_inv_cancel₀ hg, C_1, one_mul]

omit [Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ] in
private lemma exists_int_polynomial_of_primitive_factor_aux {U : MvPolynomial τ ℤ}
    {V' : Polynomial ℚ} {F : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℤ} (hgcd : U.support.gcd U.coeff = 1)
    (hF : map (Int.castRingHom ℚ) F = rename some (map (Int.castRingHom ℚ) U) *
      Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℚ) V') :
    ∃ V : Polynomial ℤ, V.map (Int.castRingHom ℚ) = V' := by
  apply (Polynomial.mem_map_range (Int.castRingHom ℚ)).2
  intro b
  obtain ⟨z, hz⟩ := exists_int_eq_of_gcd_eq_one hgcd fun a _ ↦
    ⟨F.coeff (a.mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none b), by
      have h := congrArg (fun P ↦ P.coeff (a.mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none b)) hF
      simp only [coeff_rename_mul_aeval, coeff_map] at h
      simpa using h.symm⟩
  exact ⟨z, hz.symm⟩

omit [Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ] in
/-- If an integer polynomial `F` factors over `ℚ` as `U'(x_τ) V'(x_none)` with nonzero factors,
then it factors over `ℤ` as `U(x_τ) V(x_none)` with the same partial degrees.
Source: §2, proof of Lemma 2 ("Thus `U, V` are integral and nonzero, as required"), together
with the degree comparison at the end of the proof. -/
theorem exists_int_factors {U' : MvPolynomial τ ℚ} {V' : Polynomial ℚ} (hU : U' ≠ 0) (hV : V' ≠ 0)
    {F : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℤ} (hF : map (Int.castRingHom ℚ) F =
      rename some U' * Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℚ) V') :
    ∃ (U : MvPolynomial τ ℤ) (V : Polynomial ℤ), U ≠ 0 ∧ V ≠ 0 ∧
      F = rename some U * Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℤ) V ∧
      (∀ j, degreeOf j U = degreeOf j U') ∧ V.natDegree = V'.natDegree := by
  obtain ⟨g, U, hg, hUmap, hgcd⟩ := exists_primitive_smul_eq hU
  have hF' := rescale_separated_factor_aux hg hUmap hF
  obtain ⟨V, hVmap⟩ := exists_int_polynomial_of_primitive_factor_aux hgcd hF'
  refine ⟨U, V, ?_, ?_, ?_, fun j ↦ ?_, ?_⟩
  · exact fun h ↦ smul_ne_zero hg hU (by simpa [h] using hUmap.symm)
  · exact fun h ↦ mul_ne_zero (Polynomial.C_ne_zero.2 (inv_ne_zero hg)) hV
      (by simpa [h] using hVmap.symm)
  · apply MvPolynomial.map_injective (Int.castRingHom ℚ) Int.cast_injective
    rw [hF', map_mul (map (Int.castRingHom ℚ)), map_rename,
      Polynomial.map_aeval_eq_aeval_map (φ := Int.castRingHom ℚ) (Subsingleton.elim _ _), map_X,
      hVmap]
  · rw [degreeOf, ← degrees_map_of_injective U (f := Int.castRingHom ℚ) Int.cast_injective,
      hUmap, ← degreeOf, smul_eq_C_mul, degreeOf_C_mul j g (mem_nonZeroDivisors_of_ne_zero hg)]
  · rw [← Polynomial.natDegree_map_eq_of_injective (f := Int.castRingHom ℚ)
      (RingHom.injective_int _), hVmap, Polynomial.natDegree_C_mul (inv_ne_zero hg)]

end Integrality

omit [Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ] in
private lemma exists_rat_factors_det_hasseDeriv_aux [Finite τ] {P : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℤ}
    (hP : P ≠ 0) {n : ℕ} (hn : degreeOf none P ≤ n) : ∃ l, 1 ≤ l ∧ l ≤ n + 1 ∧
      ∃ D : Fin l → (τ →₀ ℕ), (∀ μ, (D μ).degree ≤ μ) ∧ ∃ (U : MvPolynomial τ ℚ) (V : Polynomial ℚ),
        U ≠ 0 ∧ V ≠ 0 ∧ map (Int.castRingHom ℚ) (Matrix.of fun μ ν : Fin l ↦
          hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) P).det = rename some U *
          Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℚ) V := by
  classical
  set PQ := map (Int.castRingHom ℚ) P with hPQdef
  have hdeg : degreeOf none PQ ≤ n := by
    simpa [degreeOf_def, hPQdef,
      degrees_map_of_injective P (f := Int.castRingHom ℚ) Int.cast_injective] using hn
  obtain ⟨l, hl1, hl2, φ, ψ, hφ, hψ, -, -, hPQ⟩ :=
    exists_separated_representation (fun h ↦
      hP (MvPolynomial.map_injective _ Int.cast_injective (h.trans (map_zero _).symm))) hdeg
  obtain ⟨D, hD, hG⟩ := exists_genWronskian_ne_zero hψ
  set F := (Matrix.of fun μ ν : Fin l ↦
    hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) P).det with hFdef
  have hFmap : map (Int.castRingHom ℚ) F = rename some (genWronskian D ψ) *
      Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℚ) (wronskian φ) := by
    rw [hFdef, RingHom.map_det, ← det_hasseDeriv_separated D φ ψ, ← hPQ]
    congr 1
    refine Matrix.ext fun μ ν ↦ ?_
    rw [RingHom.mapMatrix_apply, Matrix.map_apply, Matrix.of_apply, Matrix.of_apply,
      hasseDeriv_map]
  exact ⟨l, hl1, hl2, D, hD, _, _, hG, wronskian_ne_zero_of_linearIndependent hφ, hFmap⟩

omit [Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ] in
/-- Lemma 2. Source: §2, Lemma `roth:lem2` ("there is an integer `l` satisfying `1 ≤ l ≤ r_p + 1`
and there are differential operators `Δ_0, …, Δ_{l-1}` on the variables `x_1, …, x_{p-1}` of
orders at most `0, …, l-1` such that ... `F = U(x_1, …, x_{p-1}) V(x_p)` where `U, V` are nonzero,
have integral coefficients, `U` is of degree at most `l r_j` in `x_j` ... and `V` is of degree
at most `l r_p`"). -/
theorem exists_det_eq_mul [Finite τ] {P : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℤ} (hP : P ≠ 0) {r : Option τ → ℕ}
    (hr : ∀ j, degreeOf j P ≤ r j) : ∃ l, 1 ≤ l ∧ l ≤ r none + 1 ∧ ∃ D : Fin l → (τ →₀ ℕ),
      (∀ μ, (D μ).degree ≤ μ) ∧ ∃ (U : MvPolynomial τ ℤ) (V : Polynomial ℤ), U ≠ 0 ∧ V ≠ 0 ∧
        (∀ j, degreeOf j U ≤ l * r (some j)) ∧ V.natDegree ≤ l * r none ∧
        (Matrix.of fun μ ν : Fin l ↦
            hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) P).det = rename some U *
          Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℤ) V := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨l, hl1, hl2, D, hD, U', V', hU', hV', hFmap⟩ :=
    exists_rat_factors_det_hasseDeriv_aux hP (hr none)
  obtain ⟨U, V, hU, hV, hFUV, -, -⟩ := exists_int_factors hU' hV' hFmap
  refine ⟨l, hl1, hl2, D, hD, U, V, hU, hV, fun j ↦ ?_, ?_, hFUV⟩
  · rw [← degreeOf_rename_mul_aeval U hV j, ← hFUV]
    exact degreeOf_det_hasseDeriv_le
      (fun μ ν : Fin l ↦ (D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) P hr (some j)
  · rw [← degreeOf_none_rename_mul_aeval hU V, ← hFUV]
    exact degreeOf_det_hasseDeriv_le
      (fun μ ν : Fin l ↦ (D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (ν : ℕ)) P hr none

end SeparationPrivate165

    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.MainLemma `Roth.exists_auxiliary_polynomial_of_log_bound_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.MainLemma `Roth.theta_lt_eta_of_sq_log_bound_aux,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.MainLemma `Roth.exists_auxiliary_polynomial_with_index_gap_aux,
    `Roth.main_lemma,
    `Roth.approxSet,
    `Roth.exists_nat_mul_isIntegral,
    `Roth.approxSet_den_le_finite,

    `Roth.reduction,
    `Roth.exists_monic_aeval_eq_zero,
    Lean.mkPrivateNameCore `Roth.Main `Roth.approxSet_num_den_mem_aux,
    `Roth.exists_reduced_approx,
    `Roth.exists_m,
